不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
科技股漲多修正，資金輪動主導盤勢
2026 年 8 月
7 月全球股市呈現鮮明的區域輪動 ， 資金由先前漲幅集中的亞洲AI 供應鏈 ， 轉向估值與今年以來
表現相對落後的市場。以美元計價觀察， MSCI 中國、東協及拉丁美洲為當月前三強。相較之下，
MSCI 新興亞洲與新興市場跌幅較大，MSCI 新興亞洲中南韓與台灣合計權重接近六成，7 月南韓半
導體及 AI 硬體股因估值偏高 、 資本支出回收疑慮與槓桿部位平倉而急跌 ， 成為指數的主要壓力來源。
即便 7 月新興亞洲與新興市場出現回檔，但年初迄今表現仍佳，反觀中國與印度仍分別下跌 8.0%及

9.0%。因此，我們認為 7 月盤勢較接近強勢 AI 題材暫時性獲利了結，而先前落後市場低基期補漲的
資金輪動行情，尚不足以判定全球股市原有多頭結構已全面反轉。
七月全球債市整體偏弱，僅新興當地債及亞洲高收益債維持正報酬。以美元計價觀察，新興美元
債下跌 1.30% 為最弱券種。當月債市壓力主要來自能源價格波動、通膨黏著性及聯準會政策不確定
性。Fed 於 7/29 決議將利率維持在 3.50%至 3.75%，但三位地區聯準銀行總裁主張立即升息一碼，
官方聲明亦明確指出通膨仍高於 2%目標，能源供給衝擊持續推升部分價格。當日會後 30 年期美債
殖利率創 2007 年以來高位，但 2 年期殖利率卻下降，反映市場並非單純押注短期升息，而是提高對
長期通膨、政策可信度與期限溢酬的要求，殖利率曲線因而明顯陡峭化。
我們認為，七月股市修正主要來自折現率、評價與籌碼調整，目前尚未出現AI 需求下降的證據。
半導體類股近期修正後，評價壓力已部分釋放，但技術面仍未形成明確底部。既有核心持股不宜因短
線波動而全面減碼，但建議降低高評價、高擁擠度及高度依賴遠期獲利族群的集中度。此外，投資人

可以定時定額或在拉回時分批布局具獲利潛力的台、日股市。韓國股市則受三星電子、SK 海力士雙
巨頭合計占指數權重逾半，個股風險易被結構性放大，建議投資人於短線反彈適度調節相關曝險。
在聯準會減少前瞻指引、利率路徑更依賴數據環境下，市場對聯準會政策預期的調整頻率將提
高，預期債券市場波動性亦將隨之升溫。投資人對 Fed 升息疑慮或導致短天期債券殖利率出現較大
波動，且存續期間過短亦面臨再投資風險；長天期債券又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給
增加等負面因素。因此，建議債券布局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸
引力的息收，亦能降低短端政策波動及長端期限溢酬風險。

美國股市：AI 成長主線未變，短線估值風險升溫
七月美股受伊朗戰爭不確定性、 聯準會態度轉鷹干擾 ，股市出現巨幅震盪 。 雖財報盈餘表現強勁，
但市場開始觀察資本支出擴張壓迫科技龍頭企業的現金流。指數上揚主要由微軟、蘋果、亞馬遜、輝
達、埃克森美孚、博通、摩根大通銀行、雪佛龍、萬事達卡、威士卡等企業帶動，表現相對弱勢的族
群包括美光、特斯拉、英特爾、應用材料、科林研發、科磊、晟碟、超微半導體、卡特彼勒、康寧等。
美國經濟整體仍維持穩健 ， 但第二季GDP 年化季增率由 2.1%放緩至 1.5%， 主要反映進口增加、
企業庫存調整及政府支出下降。民間消費仍是第二季經濟成長的主要支撐，年化季增率由第一季的

0.5%大幅回升至 3.2%，其中耐久財消費成長 6.8%、非耐久財成長 4.4%，服務消費亦由 0.5%回升
至 2.2%。此外，第二季民間投資成長3.0%，其中非住宅投資成長 8.4%，顯示 AI 資料中心、軟體與
研發支出仍持續擴張。另一方面，第二季進口大幅成長11.5%，增幅主要集中於電信設備、半導體及
工業設備等資本財，反映企業資本支出需求仍相當強勁。
能源價格回落有助通膨降溫，但整體物價壓力仍不容忽視。6 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 月增率同步放
緩 ， 其中整體PCE 轉為月減 0.1%， 反映能源價格明顯回落 ， 足以抵銷其他商品與服務價格上漲壓力，
年增率為 3.7%，仍高於 Fed 目標。核心 PCE 月增率亦降至 0.1%，創近數月低點；其中，聯準會高
度關注的超級核心服務通膨出現降溫 ， 主要受投資組合管理費漲幅由4.5%大幅降至 0.6%， 以及旅館
住宿與機票價格回落帶動。不過，醫療保健價格仍維持穩健增長，且部分商品價格持續受到關稅影響
而上揚，加上美伊戰事仍未落幕，通膨風險依舊存在。

本輪由 AI 投資帶動的「AI 通膨」 ，對整體CPI 的直接推升有限，卻有助於川普推動「美國製造」
政策目標。2024 年以來 ， 全球雲端服務業者大幅擴張AI 資本支出 ， 帶動高階晶片 、記憶體需求急升，
不過，電視、電腦周邊及手機合計占美國CPI 權重偏低，即使相關產品價格上漲，對整體通膨的直接
影響仍相對有限。相較之下，AI 通膨對產業結構與供應鏈在地化的影響更值得關注。台積電毛利率
上升同時也擴大在美國資本支出 、美光也在記憶體價格回升後重新啟動紐約廠建設、輝達則進一步投
資光通訊與先進封裝業者 ， 協助相關產能在美國落地 。 唯有當價格與需求長期維持在足以支撐獲利的
水準，企業才有能力承擔高額資本支出，並推動製造業供應鏈回美擴張。
目前標普 500 指數續創新高，本益比已升至 25.8 倍、股價淨值比達 5.8 倍，短期評價偏高。雖
然美國科技業者目前仍居 AI 產業領先地位，但AI 技術與競爭格局變化快速，領先廠商尚未與後續追
趕者明顯拉開差距。短期仍需留意市場過熱與估值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：經濟溫和復甦，評價仍具吸引力
七月歐股受伊朗戰爭不確定性干擾而震盪。泛歐 600 指數中，能源與金融股表現相對強勢，包括
殼牌、匯豐、道達爾能源、BP、畢爾巴鄂比斯開銀行、聯合聖保羅銀行及法國巴黎銀行等；相較之
下 ， 半導體 、 科技硬體與部分防禦型消費股表現偏弱 ， 包括艾司摩爾 、 英飛凌 、 意法半導體、諾基亞、
雀巢等。整體市場焦點由科技股回歸能源與金融族群。
歐元區第 2 季 GDP 初值季增率回升至 0.4%，顯示經濟動能在第 1 季停滯後逐步修復。不過，成
員國表現仍明顯分化：西班牙季增 0.7%，持續領先主要經濟體，主要受惠於服務業復甦與觀光需求

回升；德國 GDP 季增率則放緩至 0.2%，反映製造業疲弱與出口需求不足仍限制復甦力道；法國則
由前季萎縮轉為成長 0.2%，顯示內需逐步改善。整體而言，第 2 季 GDP 回升主要受消費改善、投
資回穩及政府支出支撐，但復甦力道仍屬溫和。
7 月歐元區通膨率受能源價格反彈影響 ， 小幅回升至2.9%， 已連續5 個月高於 ECB 的 2.0%目標 ，
但仍低於 5 月 3.2%的高點。核心通膨則升至 2.5%，主要受服務業與非能源工業品價格同步走揚帶
動，其中服務業通膨由3.2%升至 3.3%，非能源工業品由 0.7%升至 0.9%；食品與菸酒年增率則續降
至 1.2%，反映食品價格降溫趨勢延續。整體而言，物價壓力僅溫和回升，尚未出現全面加速跡象。
ECB 在七月利率決策會議維持政策利率 2.25%不變，重申將採取逐次會議、數據依賴的決策模
式，不預先承諾未來利率路徑，官方仍維持經濟成長風險偏下行的評估。雖然歐元區整體通膨較前期
高點放緩 ， 但中東衝突再度升溫 ， 推升原油與天然氣價格 ， 加上目前全球能源與天然氣庫存水位偏低 ，

未來能源價格變化及其對通膨的傳導效果，仍將是市場關注重點。ECB 刻意未恢復前瞻指引，並持續
強調後續數據的重要性 ， 反映其希望在高度不確定環境下保留政策彈性 ， 因此不排除9 月升息的可能 。
7 月歐元區綜合 PMI 回升至 51.9，重返榮枯線之上並創 5 個月新高，整體景氣動能有所改善。
其中，服務業 PMI 升至 51.6，受惠於需求回升而重返擴張區間；製造業 PMI 亦由 51.4 升至 51.9，
加上新訂單 5 個月來首度恢復成長，進一步支撐景氣修復。
歐洲經濟整體呈現溫和復甦 ， 泛歐600 指數成分股多為具備全球布局優勢的產業龍頭 。 目前指數
本益比約 17.5 倍、股價淨值比約 2.5 倍，評價仍屬合理。雖然能源價格高檔與通膨再升溫風險仍需
留意，但在評價合理、伊朗戰爭有望於第三季落幕的背景下，歐股仍適合作為長線分批布局標的。

日本股市：日圓匯率短線急升，股市評價合理
七月日股受地緣政治風險與全球科技股回檔影響，日經 225 指數劇烈震盪。盤面上，金融、外銷
與大型權值股表現相對強勢，包括三菱 UFJ 金融集團、日立製作所、索尼、豐田、三井住友金融、
東京海上控股、瑞穗金融、三菱商事等；半導體與電子零組件族群則相對承壓，包括東京威力科創、
村田製作所、鎧俠、瑞薩、Disco、信越化學等。整體市場焦點由科技股轉向外銷股與升息預期受惠
的金融股。
7 月東京 CPI 年增率自前月的 1.7%攀升至 2.0%、東京核心 CPI（扣除生鮮食品） 年增率為1.9%、

扣除食品與能源後的 CPI 年增率則為 1.3%。商品價格年增率由前月的 2.3%升至 2.9%，是通膨回升
的主要原因；服務價格年增率則小幅升至 1.2%，顯示薪資成長仍推升服務價格。分項來看，本月通
膨上行主要來自能源與公用事業費用，反映電費與瓦斯費價格上漲；同時，食品價格年增率也由前月
的 3.8%回升至 4.0%。展望後市，日本政府 7~9 月重啟電費補貼，將抑制能源價格。不過，近期國
際油價再度攀高，可能抵消部分政策效果。
日本央行七月利率決策會議維持政策利率不變，委員以 8 比 1 同意將政策利率維持於 1.0%。僅
高田創委員主張將政策利率調高至 1.25%。就景氣現況來看，日本經濟仍呈現溫和復甦態勢，儘管部
分經濟活動受到中東局勢影響而略顯疲弱，但出口與工業生產大致保持平穩。受惠於全球 AI 相關需
求強勁成長，企業固定投資仍溫和增加；同時，私人消費在就業與所得環境改善支撐下仍具韌性。日
銀也將 2026 財年 GDP 年增率預估由前次的 0.5%小幅上調至 0.6%，2027 財年由 0.7%上調至 0.8%。

近期美日聯手干預日圓匯率，日本財務大臣片山皋月表示，已協同美國財政部實施買進日圓的
干預措施，以因應近來日圓匯率過度波動及失序走勢，且不排除進一步採取協同干預。美國財長貝森
特也表示，將毫不猶豫參與進一步的聯合日圓干預行動，並強烈支持日本採取果斷措施，修正日圓嚴
重低估的問題。美國總統川普則稱，日本需要一些幫助。此次為日本與美國 15 年來首次就日圓採取
聯合行動，兩國共同干預匯市確實對日圓形成支撐，突襲式干預也有助引發投資人空單回補，暫時延
續日圓升值趨勢。不過，目前日美政策利率差距仍高達 2.5%，若日本央行無法加快升息步伐，中長
期匯價重新回歸利差基本面，日圓仍可能重拾偏弱格局。
2026 年來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材 、 高市擴大財政刺激與強化軍事安全等政策利多支撐，
儘管指數已明顯上漲，目前本益比約 19.5 倍、股價淨值比約 1.8 倍，評價仍屬合理，可利用市場震
盪機會逢低分批布局。

台灣股市：AI 出口與接單動能續強，8 月聚焦財報兌現與供應鏈擴散
7 月台股因前期漲幅較大、科技股評價偏高，加上市場重新檢視AI 投資回收期、企業獲利能否符
合高預期，外資調節、融資去化與槓桿資金停損進一步放大波動，資金也由高評價電子股轉向較具防
禦性的族群。不過，這波修正有助消化過高評價與浮動籌碼，降低市場過熱風險；後續隨雲端業者財
報持續驗證 AI 資本支出，半導體、伺服器及先進封裝需求延續，市場焦點可望逐步回歸企業獲利與
產業基本面 。6 月景氣對策信號升至 41 分 、 連續第7 個月亮紅燈 ， 製造業銷售與加班工時同步轉強，

顯示 AI 動能已由出口延伸至生產、設備投資與勞動投入。雖然產業間景氣感受仍有落差，但下半年
電子產品備貨需求、半導體資本支出及 AI 基礎建設持續推進，仍可望支撐出口與企業獲利，為後續
盤勢提供正向動能。
當前臺灣經濟正處於生產擴張的黃金期。最新公布的總體經濟數據與半導體龍頭營收呈現全面共
振，印證全球 AI 資本支出（CapEx）並非流於故事題材，而是實質轉化為台廠的訂單與獲利。儘管
全球金融市場短期受到地緣政治干擾與美債殖利率波動影響 ， 引發台股在高檔震盪 ， 但主計總處大幅
上修 2026 年 GDP 預測至 9.64%。國發會公布 6 月景氣對策信號綜合判斷分數為 41 分，較 5 月
增加 2 分，連續第 7 個月呈現代表景氣熱絡的紅燈，為史上第二長的連續紅燈紀錄，顯示台灣景氣
並非單月跳升，而是已形成一段相對持續的高檔擴張週期。
展望 8 月，台灣景氣仍有望維持高檔，但景氣分數的邊際變化將比燈號本身更值得關注。全球
AI 基礎建設持續擴張，加上下半年進入消費性電子備貨旺季，預期有利半導體、AI 伺服器、先進封

裝、散熱、電源及高速傳輸供應鏈；國內主要半導體業者提高資本支出，也將持續帶動設備、材料及
廠務工程需求。另一方面，景氣分數已接近紅燈區間上緣 45 分，未來即使出口與生產維持強勁，受
到比較基期墊高影響，分數未必持續上升，因此不宜將分數持平或小幅回落直接解讀為景氣反轉。
投資策略上可維持對 AI 供應鏈的正向看法，但選股宜從題材轉向獲利品質。除先進製程外，8
月可提高對先進封裝、測試介面、AI 伺服器組裝、散熱、及記憶體的關注。IDC 預估 2026 年全球半
導體市場規模將達 1.29 兆美元、年增52.8%，資料中心半導體營收達 4,771 億美元，意味著AI 投資
正由 GPU 延伸至記憶體、網路與系統級零組件。不過，若股價已提前反映高成長，8 月仍應留意財
測不及預期、匯率、成本上升及資金集中度過高所引發的波動。

中國股市：復甦力道偏緩，科技自主為結構性題材但仍待驗證
7 月中國經濟延續 「總量放緩 、 科技製造相對抗跌」 的分化格局 。2026 年上半年 GDP 年增 4.7%，
第二季成長率由第一季的 5.0%放緩至 4.3%；6 月規模以上工業增加值年增 5.3%，其中製造業成長
6.0%，電腦、通信及其他電子設備製造業成長 15.7%。數據顯示 AI 與電子設備提供局部支撐，但內
需疲弱與房地產調整仍未見明顯改善，整體復甦動能偏緩，成長結構對少數產業的依賴度偏高。
科技產業方面，7 月市場焦點轉向半導體設備、AI 模型與記憶體的自主化，惟題材面成分仍高於

基本面。據媒體報導，中國已開始製造國產浸沒式 DUV 微影設備，首批系統預計供應中芯國際、華
虹半導體及長鑫科技 ， 但2026 年產量可能僅約 5 台 ， 且效能 、 可靠度與量產驗證仍明顯落後ASML，
短期象徵意義大於實際產能貢獻 。 後續能否降低成熟製程與部分多重曝光製程對進口設備的依賴 ， 仍
取決於良率與客戶端驗證結果，對國產蝕刻、薄膜沉積、檢測及零組件的實質拉動亦需時間觀察。
AI 軟體端，月之暗面於 7 月推出 Kimi K3，模型具 2.8 兆參數、100 萬 Token 上下文及原生視覺
理解能力，並採開放權重策略，反映中國大模型競爭已由低價推論延伸至模型能力與開發者生態。惟
開放權重的商業模式尚未證明可持續獲利，應用落地速度亦存在不確定性。若相關應用推展順利，或
有利國產雲端服務 、 資料中心 、 伺服器與推論晶片需求 ； 但模型效率提升同時可能壓抑算力採購規模 ，
市場對 AI 投資的檢視標準將轉向使用效率與變現能力。
記憶體方面 ， 長鑫科技正由一般DRAM 逐步向 DDR5、LPDDR5X 及 HBM3 延伸 ， 市場研究 「估

計」 其2026 年底 DRAM 月產能可能接近 35 萬片晶圓，惟該預估未經證實，實際良率與產品組合仍
待確認 。 國產DUV 若逐步導入其產線 ， 或可提高設備取得的自主性 ， 但導入時程與穩定度均具變數。
整體而言，8 月中國市場可留意半導體設備、國產記憶體、AI 模型、資料中心與工業自動化，惟評價
已部分反映預期，投資判斷宜以技術驗證、訂單落地與實際營收貢獻為依據，避免過度追逐自主替代
題材。
展望 8 月，政策環境預計仍以穩增長及培育新動能為主，但政策效果傳導有限，市場表現料將持
續分化。中國上半年新動能，包括高端製造、數位經濟與現代服務業，對經濟成長的貢獻超過四成；
第三季製造業雖有 AI 相關產品、綠色科技出口及政策支持，惟房地產、基礎建設投資及中小企業需
求疲弱仍是主要壓抑因素。

拉美市場：巴西通膨改善支撐市場表現，惟總統大選不確定性仍存
7 月全球股市普遍面臨回檔，然而拉美股市表現卻相對抗跌，主因巴西市場先前已因降息預期收
斂而出現修正，而近期巴西通膨數據及通膨預期出現改善，使市場對於巴西通膨問題的擔憂有所緩
解，也帶動巴西指數表現相對硬挺，並為整體拉美市場指數提供支撐。
7 月巴西通膨數據出現改善，主要來自食品與燃料價格回落。7 月巴西 IPCA-15 消費者物價指
數月增 0.06%，明顯低於市場預期的 0.22%，年增率亦由 6 月的 4.80% 降至 4.52%。其中，家庭
食品價格由 6 月月增 0.87% 轉為月減 1.14%；燃料價格整體月減 0.90%， 汽油價格月減 0.68%，

帶動整體通膨放緩，並降低市場對近期巴西物價再度急升的疑慮。
此外，本次巴西通膨數據改善亦反映在核心通膨與服務通膨上，使優於預期的數據更接近結構性
通膨改善。五種核心通膨指標平均月增 0.21%，年增率由 4.45% 降至 4.35%。同時，服務通膨月
增 0.41%，低於市場預期的 0.59%，年增率也由 6.25% 降至 5.95%；然而，勞力密集型服務年增
率仍由 7.06% 升至 7.30%，顯示通膨壓力尚未全面消退，後續仍需持續觀察。
另一方面，儘管巴西通膨數據改善 ， 仍未逆轉市場對巴西央行降息幅度受限的預期。截至 7 月 27
日的巴西央行每週市場調查顯示，2026 年市場通膨預期較前期下降 3 個基點至 5.12%，但 2027
年仍上升 2 個基點至 4.22%，2028 年亦連續第二週上升 2 個基點至 3.80%，2029 年則維持
3.50%；四個年度皆持續高於巴西央行 3.0%的通膨目標 。此外 ，市場對 2026、2027、2028 及 2029
年底巴西基準利率的預期，亦分別維持在 14.00%、12.00%、10.50%及 10.00%，顯示市場對通膨

的疑慮仍限制巴西利率正常化的速度與幅度。
除了通膨之外，巴西總統大選是影響拉美市場的另一項關鍵因素。巴西民調機構 Nexus 於 7 月
全國民調顯示，在總統大選第一輪中，魯拉以 42% 領先波索納洛的 33%，與 7 月中旬前次調查
的 42% 對 34% 大致相同；第二輪則為 47% 對 43%，魯拉仍領先 4 個百分點。兩輪調查均由
魯拉領先，顯示其整體勝率仍較高。此外，包含 Datafolha、Genial／Quaest 等民調皆顯示目前魯
拉贏面較高。由於魯拉並非市場偏好的候選人，其較高的連任機率可能壓抑巴西股市表現。因此，儘
管近期巴西通膨降溫有助支撐整體拉美市場行情，巴西選舉風險仍可能抵銷部分利多；同時，在美伊
衝突仍具不確定性的情況下，巴西降息行情亦料將受到壓抑，後市表現預期將落後於全球市場。

科技類股：CSP 廠商資本支出持續加碼，市場更加關注獲利轉化能力
全球科技產業的主要成長動能仍集中於 AI 基礎建設，四大雲端服務供應商資本支出均維持高檔
或進一步上修，顯示資料中心、AI 伺服器、網路設備及雲端容量的擴建週期尚未降溫。Microsoft、
Amazon 與 Google 的雲端業務均維持強勁成長，且訂單、產能需求與客戶使用量持續增加，反映企
業導入生成式 AI、主權 AI 及 AI 工廠的趨勢仍在深化。市場焦點也逐漸由單一 AI 晶片出貨，延伸至
高速網路、記憶體、CPU、電力與散熱等整體資料中心架構。只要 CSP 廠商未明顯下修資本支出，

AI 硬體供應鏈的訂單能見度便可望維持，NVIDIA、AMD 及相關半導體與設備廠商仍是本輪科技投
資循環的主要受惠者。
不過，市場對科技公司的評價標準正由投入多少資本支出轉向能否將投資轉化為營收、獲利與現
金流。Microsoft 在大幅增加資本支出的同時，營運現金流與自由現金流仍維持成長，Google 雲端
業務的獲利也明顯提升 ， 說明部分AI 投資已逐步進入商業化階段 ；Amazon 則受惠 AWS 成長加速，
雲端業務持續扮演主要獲利來源。相較之下，Meta 與特斯拉因 AI 研發、基礎建設及新事業投入快
速增加，面臨費用上升、利潤率下降與自由現金流承壓的問題。未來科技股表現可能進一步分化，市
場將更重視 AI 收入成長 、 產能利用率 、 投資回收期及折舊費用 ， 而非單純將高額資本支出視為利多。
從供應鏈角度來看，AI 需求正由 GPU 逐步擴散至 CPU、記憶體、晶圓代工、類比晶片與工業半
導體。Intel 資料中心與 AI 業務明顯加速，顯示 CPU 在 AI 伺服器的資料處理、系統控制與通用運算

架構中仍具重要地位。記憶體價格上升雖有利於相關製造商，但也推高 CSP 建置成本，促使雲端業
者重新檢視採購效率與系統配置。整體而言，科技產業有機會由少數 AI 核心企業領漲，逐步轉為伺
服器、網通、儲存、電源、散熱及工業半導體共同受惠的擴散型成長。
展望後續，AI 仍是科技產業最具確定性的投資主軸，但高成長也伴隨高估值與高資本密集度風
險。NVIDIA 的 Blackwell 出貨、Rubin 平台時程、高階記憶體供應與中國市場需求，將是觀察 AI
硬體循環的重要指標；Apple、Meta 與特斯拉則需證明 AI 功能、新裝置及自動化應用能夠轉化為實
際收入。目前 CSP 訂單與雲端需求仍未出現明顯降溫，科技產業基本面維持正向，但隨著折舊、能
源、記憶體及研發成本陸續上升，企業獲利差異可能擴大。未來較具優勢的企業，將不只是擁有先進
技術或積極投入 AI， 而是能建立穩定商業模式 ， 並將AI 投資有效轉化為營收 、 利潤與現金流的公司。

能源類股：美伊衝突帶動油價波動，實體供應仍未見改善
7 月油價波動劇烈，主因美伊衝突與停火談判反覆。美伊在 7 月中旬恢復互相攻擊、美國重啟
對伊朗港口的海上封鎖，且荷莫茲海峽再度發生商船遇襲事件，帶動布蘭特原油一度重返每桶 100
美元以上；然而其後美方暫停攻擊並重啟外交談判後，油價又回落至約 80 美元，這顯示當前油價
變動主要反映市場對於衝突升降溫的預期，而非實際供應已恢復正常。
彭博分析指出，目前石油實物市場尚未出現明顯改善，荷莫茲海峽可觀察到的油輪數量仍遠低於
戰前，許多船隻通行時仍持續關閉船舶自動識別系統，且保險公司、租船人及船東也維持審慎態度，

顯示商業航運、海運保險、運費及出口流量均未恢復正常。航運資料公司 Windward 7 月 30 日的
船舶追蹤也顯示，荷莫茲海峽當日僅有 7 艘船舶進入波斯灣、4 艘離開，整體運量相較於戰前仍有
極大的落差，故一旦美伊談判受阻，地緣政治風險溢價仍有迅速回升的可能性。
另一方面，胡塞武裝宣布將封鎖沙烏地阿拉伯有關的船舶運輸，並對紅海曼德海峽通行發出威
脅，也加劇油價上行風險。彭博指出，沙烏地阿拉伯雖可利用東西輸油管，將原先在荷莫茲海峽受阻
的每日約 500 萬桶原油運往紅海沿岸的延布港，但經由紅海運往亞洲的船舶仍須通過曼德海峽，因
此原油出口風險只是由波斯灣轉移至紅海。船舶追蹤公司 Windward 截至 8 月 3 日的統計顯示，
7 月 20 日後曼德海峽船隻流量下降 22%， 油輪更減少 40%，顯示經紅海的替代路線仍面臨威脅。
然而 ， 高油價帶來的需求破壞也會限制油價上漲空間 。OPEC 7 月月報將 2026 年全球石油需求
量下修至每日 1.0594 億桶，需求年增量由每日 97 萬桶降至 78 萬桶；2027 年需求年增量雖上

修至每日 194 萬桶，但上修主因 2026 年基期較低的緣故，並非終端需求出現改善。
在此背景下，彭博研究將油價分為三種情境：若外交協議落地、荷莫茲航運與保險恢復正常，布
蘭特原油可能回到每桶 65 至 80 美元；若戰事僅暫停、實物供應持續受阻，則較符合每桶 90 至
110 美元的衝突僵局情境；若荷莫茲與曼德海峽同時受阻，油價可能升至每桶 140 美元以上。
展望 8 月，川普宣布將和伊朗展開新一輪談判，儘管隨即遭到伊朗否認，且荷莫茲海峽仍處於受阻
狀態 ， 然而我們認為隨著期中選舉時程逼近 ， 川普將可能暫緩對伊朗的行動 ， 藉此壓低油價挽救選情 ，
在此情境下能源類股將會轉趨區間震盪整理，目前中性看待後市表現。

身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：央行配置需求仍強，惟 ETF 資金流出壓抑短線表現
7 月金價保持在區間內震盪整理，儘管美元走強及美債殖利率攀升持續為金價帶來壓力，然而來
自全球央行的買盤也如我們先前所料的為金價帶來了有力的支撐。
世界黃金協會報告指出，2026 年第二季，全球黃金總需求為 1,269 噸，與 2025 年同期持平；
累計 2026 年上半年，全球黃金總需求年增 2%至 2,522 噸，需求金額創下 3,800 億美元新高。與此
同時 ，2026 年第二季倫敦金銀市場協會黃金午後定盤價平均為每盎司 4,506.29 美元 ， 雖較第一季下

跌 8%，但仍較 2025 年同期上漲 37%。這顯示第二季金價雖自高檔回落，但全球黃金需求並未同步
轉弱，尤其央行買盤回升更成為支撐市場的主要力量。
第二季全球央行合計購買 289 噸黃金，歷經第一季的放緩後，購金規模再次重新回到過去四年
的高檔水準。高盛分析則預估，全球央行 5 月購金量達到 81 噸，其中中國購買 48 噸，是當月最大
的可識別買家；經季節調整後，全球央行近三個月月均購金量達到 67 噸，約為 2022 年前每月 17
噸平均值的四倍 。 上述數據顯示 ， 即使在金價走勢低迷的狀況下，全球央行對於黃金的承接力道仍強 。
進一步觀察央行持續購金的原因，官方貨幣與金融機構論壇（OMFIF）2026 年針對 74 家央行
及 16 家公共退休與主權基金進行的調查顯示，儲備多元化仍是央行持有黃金最主要的原因。此外，
有 51%的受訪者將防範地緣政治風險列為考量，比例較 2024 年增加 11 個百分點；同時，79%的受
訪央行認為全球貨幣體系正朝向多極化發展 。調查結果顯示， 各國央行正逐步降低對單一儲備貨幣的

依賴，並增持黃金以因應制裁、資產凍結及貨幣體系分裂風險，由此也可見，本波全球央行買盤更接
近長期儲備調整，而非短線對於金價的追逐。
然而對於利率較為敏感的私人投資資金 ， 降低黃金配置趨勢仍持續為金價帶來明顯壓力。 世界黃
金協會數據顯示，2026 年第二季全球黃金 ETF 淨流出 45 噸，6 月單月再流出 89 億美元；其中，北
美黃金 ETF 上半年累計流出 77 億美元。造成北美資金流出的主因，在於聯準會態度偏向鷹派，加上
美伊衝突推升通膨疑慮，帶動實質利率與美元走高，進而提高持有黃金的機會成本。
展望後市，儘管短線上金價表現持續承壓，然而中長線看，當前市場已定價到明年底前 Fed 將
升息 2 碼的鷹派走勢，若後續美伊衝突有所緩和，使市場升息預期降溫，黃金 ETF 資金賣壓有望出
現改善， 屆時搭配央行長期買盤 ，黃金仍有望重啟新一波漲勢，故我們對黃金類股後市維持中性看法 。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：Fed 鷹派暫停推升長率，投資仍以 3~10 年期美債/投級債為主
FOMC 7/29 以 9 比 3 票將聯邦基金利率目標區間維持於 3.50%～3.75%。Hammack、Kashkari
及 Logan 三名委員反對維持利率不變，主張本次即升息 1 碼。會後聲明指出美國經濟活動仍以穩健
速度擴張，資本投資及生產力成長強勁，但通膨仍高於 2%目標，部分反映能源等供給衝擊。
Fed 主席 Warsh 強調，本次按兵不動並非聯準會行動遲緩或政策慣性，並重申不存在高於 2%
的 「軟性通膨目標」 。超過五年的高通膨不可能因單月物價小幅下降便獲得解決，聯準會將持續履行

物價穩定責任，必要時不會猶豫採取行動。華許同時指出，距離上次會議的 42 天內，美債殖利率曲
線各期限的名目與實質殖利率均明顯上升，部分升幅已位居近 20 年的前十分位數。他認為，聯準會
減少前瞻指引後，市場開始依據經濟數據自行調整價格。此番談話並非表示聯準會受制於市場，而是
暗示金融市場利率上升已產生部分緊縮效果，使本次不必立即升息。華許指出，AI 相關高科技設備
及軟體投資近四季增長率接近 20%，有助於製造業產出及未來生產力，但記憶體、邏輯晶片及相關
AI 基礎建設價格亦同步上升。FOMC 已開始討論，相關漲價究竟只是特定產業的供給衝擊，或可能
演變為更廣泛的通膨壓力。
債市對此次 FOMC 決議的反應呈現長端領跌、殖利率曲線明顯陡峭化。此一走勢顯示，市場雖
因 Fed 未立即升息而降低短端利率壓力，卻同步提高對能源通膨、財政供給、期限溢酬及政策可信
度的風險定價。利率期貨對 9 月升息的定價在決議後一度升至約 75%，稍後回落至約 6 成，代表市
場並未排除升息，而是等待後續通膨與就業數據確認。8 月沒有例行 FOMC 利率會議，債市定價將

更加依賴經濟數據。由於 Warsh 減少前瞻指引，單一數據對升息機率及短端殖利率的影響可能較過
去放大，預期 8 月殖利率仍將維持高檔震盪。
8 月債市的核心矛盾在於「殖利率已具吸引力，但長端風險尚未完全穩定」。我們認為偏高的殖
利率可提供較佳息收及價格緩衝；若油價維持高檔、核心通膨降溫停滯或美國公債供給增加， 20～
30 年期債券仍可能承受期限溢酬上升壓力。貝萊德亦指出，目前美債實質殖利率已具吸引力，但收
益機會仍應透過存續期間控制與券種選擇取得，而非全面延長年期。
在未來聯準會減少前瞻指引 、 利率路徑更依賴數據環境下 ， 市場對聯準會政策預期的調整頻率將
提高，預期債券市場波動性亦將隨之升溫。投資人對 Fed 升息疑慮或導致短天期債券殖利率出現較
大波動，且存續期間過短亦面臨再投資風險；長天期債券又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供
給增加等風險。因此，建議債券布局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸
引力的息收，亦能降低短端政策波動及長端期限溢酬風險。

Source:                         Trendforce, Morgan Stanley Research
TV panel prices to stay  flat MoM in October: Mainstream 32", 43", 55", 65" and 75"
TV panel prices are expected to stay flat for the month of October. While panel
makers had previously notified their brands customers of potential price hikes from
4Q26 to mitigate elevated component costs, we believe that negotiations are
ongoing, as TV panel order momentum might slow down in the coming months on

seasonality and TV brands could reduce orders on higher panel prices. On the other
hand, panel makers continue to demonstrate good discipline, since Chinese panel
makers are committed to annual maintenance during the October holiday to control
output. We think it seems more likely that we will see TV panel price hikes toward
late 4Q26 when TV brands start to restock for CNY promotions. Our base-case
assumption is for the TV panel prices  to be flat QoQ in 4Q26, on a quarterly average

basis.
IT panel prices to remain flat MoM in October: Monitor and notebook panel prices
are expected to be flat MoM during the month. Both applications continue to  see
slower demand in  2H26 post the earlier restocking  in 1H26 amid concerns about
increasing component costs. That said, panel makers are under cost pressure and
reluctant to given more price concessions. On a quarterly average basis, we expect
IT panel prices to remain flat QoQ in 4Q26.

Risk/reward  less attractive: We maintain the view that the current valuation of
Taiwanese panel makers, AUO (2409.TW) at 2.0x P/B and Innolux (3481.TW) at 1.7x
P/B, have already baked in the expectations of glass in advanced packaging for
revenue contribution in 2028. However, some supply-chain players think this might
take longer, and there seem to be more suppliers from different supply chains vying
for this opportunity. In China, while expectations are also building for advanced

packaging, we have a    relative preference for BOE (000725.SZ; 1.4x P/B) over TCL
(000100.SZ; 1.7x P/B), on its scale  and faster progress in glass core substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We believe current valuations of 1.5-2.0x P/B have already priced in expectations
of glass in advanced packaging from 2H28 for the panel makers.
Nonetheless,  the risk in commercialization and competition remain key to watch.

TV Panel Prices Trended Down in 3Q26
TV panel prices started to edge up at the beginning of this year, rising 1% MoM in January,
2% MoM in February and 2% MoM in March. April, May and June were largely flat, while
July was down 2% MoM, August was down 1% MoM, and September was flat MoM,   in line
with our expectation that TV panel prices would start trending down from 3Q26.  That
said, we do not expect a significant decline as supply-side discipline should provide

downside support.

Below-seasonal Panel Shipments in 2H26
Over the past decade, TV panel shipments have typically been split 49%:51% between 1H
and 2H. In 2026, we expect the mix to skew toward 1H, as TV brands restocked ahead of
major promotional events, including the Winter Olympics in February, the FIFA World Cup
in June-July, and China's 618 shopping festival. We see no clear evidence that these events

will materially boost global TV demand, but they have historically shifted shipment timing
across quarters. As a result, we expect the restocking momentum seen since the start of
the year to fade, with panel order momentum slowing in 2H26.
Display Fab Utilization  ~85% in 3Q26
Stronger TV panel shipments supported display fab utilization of ~80% in 4Q24 and 82%
in 1Q25. Utilization declined to 78% in 2Q25 as brands adopted a more cautious stance
following earlier pull-ins and as the impact of China's trade-in program moderated.

Utilization rebounded to 80-85% in 3Q25 as brands restocked for year-end promotions
and remained at similar levels in 4Q25 despite seasonal softness as well as in 1Q26.
Industry utilization stayed broadly flat QoQ, at 80-85% in 2Q26, reflecting continued
supply discipline, followed by a slight uptick in 3Q26.
Over the medium to long term, we continue to believe the display industry is undergoing
structural change. Chinese panel makers have shifted their focus from top-line growth to

profitability, while lower government subsidies have reinforced supply discipline. We
believe investors increasingly recognize that the industry's production-to-order model can
remain sustainable, particularly as panel price cycles shortened to quarters in 2023-24 vs.
multiyear cycles in the previous decade. Consolidation actions, including LG Display's sale
of its Gen 8.5 Guangzhou fab to TCL and Sharp's closure of its Gen 10 Sakai fab, also

support a healthier supply backdrop (for more information, see Display – Structural
Changes on Supply Side Not Fairly Priced In, June 17, 2021).

### Page 5

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Risk/Reward Largely Fair
We view risk-reward as broadly balanced following the recent correction in panel stocks.
Below are our current views:
• AUO (2409.TW, Derrick Yang): We rate AUO Equal-weight with a PT of NT
$27.00, based on a 1.4x 2026e P/B multiple. While we believe the current round of
TV panel price increases is nearing an end, major panel makers should continue to
demonstrate solid supply discipline, providing downside support. We appreciate

AUO's efforts to diversify its business, but meaningful earnings contributions are
likely to take time.
• BOE (000725.SZ, Derrick Yang): We prefer BOE due to its scale advantage,
higher exposure to display and faster progress in glass core substrates. Our PT of
Rmb9.30 is based on 2.5x 2026e P/B, which we view as reasonable given our
estimated ROE of 5-8% over 2026-28 compared with BOE's average P/B of 1.1x and
ROE of 2-4% during 2023-25.
• Innolux (3481.TW, Derrick Yang): In semiconductors, our supply-chain checks

suggest that Innolux is participating in a foundry customer's glass core substrate
project. However, we do not expect meaningful contributions before 2028, and
additional competitors could enter as the industry moves toward mass production.
We maintain our Equal-weight rating and PT of NT$60.00, based on a 2.1x 2026e
P/B multiple.
• LG Display (034220.KS, Ryan Kim): Meaningful OLED revenue contribution
should increasingly improve earnings from 2H25, but potentially weaker end-

markets keep us from turning bullish on LGD. The stock trades at 0.6x 2026e P/B
vs. a historical mid-cycle level of 0.5x.
• TCL (000100.SZ, Derrick Yang): We are EW with a price target of Rmb4.70 (1.7x
2026e P/B). The stock is trading at 1.7x 2026e P/B vs. its mid-cycle average of 1.4x
since 2022.
• Greater China DDIC supply chain implications – Novatek (3034.TW, Daniel Yen):
Our Underweight thesis reflects weaker PC, smartphone and automotive demand,

gross margin pressure, and a lack of new growth drivers. That said, Novatek's
OLED DDI roadmap appears more competitive than those of its key Korean peers
in terms of both performance and cost, helping it gain share in Apple's iPhone
supply chain.

### Page 7

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Where We Could Be Wrong
• Stronger end demand: If end demand for major applications turns out to be better
than expected, there could be stronger panel shipments and less panel price
pressure.
• More stringent output control: If major panel makers collectively adjust
utilization to an extreme extent, they could mitigate pricing pressure in the case of
softer demand.
• Non-commodity display business: If the non-commodity business progresses

faster than expected, panel makers could show better financial results.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

below.

## Document 081: 261008_ms_largan

Source PDF: all report pdf/261008_ms_largan.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Update
Largan Precision  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Results Miss on Lower
GPM, but FAU/CPO
Development Well on Track
Largan reported 3Q26 net income of NT$5.182bn, down 27% YoY.
The company reported a 11% YoY revenue decline, to NT$15.651bn; gross margin
declined YoY and QoQ to 42.2% in 3Q26, lower than expected due to lower new-
project yields and structural green electricity cost hikes.
Management expects shipments to be flat MoM in October and decline

sequentially in November as order momentum is weaker-than-expected.
FAU/CPO development well on track: First FA  project passed customer
verification; expanding from FA into FAU, including coupling and potential FAU-to-
carrier assembly, with a dedicated new facility expected to support mass production
from around mid-2027. We maintain our long-term positive view on Largan's FAU/
CPO opportunities.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Largan Precision (3008.TW)
Base case: Our price target is based on an SOTP valuation, comprising the traditional business
and potential FAU opportunity. We value the traditional business using a residual income
model, consistent with our Greater China Hardware Tech coverage, assuming an 8.5% cost of
equity, a 7% net profit CAGR over 2026-36E and a 3% terminal growth rate. We separately

value the FAU business, assuming a 40% market share in 2028.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected high-end smartphone demand
n Faster-than-expected penetration of periscopes
n Stronger-than-expected CPO volume growth
Risks to Downside
n Greater-than-expected competition, which would erode Largan's ASP and gross margin
n Weaker-than-expected smartphone demand, especially for high-end models
n Market share gains and more pricing pressure from Sunny

n Weaker-than-expected CPO volume growth

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 124: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다삼성전자(005930) 분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다분기 영업이익 100조 시대가 열렸다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

ASML 罕⻅对三星、 SK 海⼒⼠全线零部件涨价 10% 。受 AI 扩产潮驱动，设备供需严重失
衡，应⽤材料等巨头纷纷跟进。晶圆⼚议价优势不再。
摘要：
全球最⼤半导体设备制造商 ASML 宣布对韩国客⼾实施零部件价格全线上调 10% ，三星电⼦与
SK 海⼒⼠已接受这⼀⽅案，涨价将于明年 1 ⽉起正式⽣效。这标志着半导体设备供应链的定价权
正加速向供应商⼀侧倾斜，⾏业成本压⼒持续扩散。
据韩国科技媒体 The Lec 报道， ASML 总部于上⽉初通过韩国法⼈向三星和 SK 海⼒⼠发出涨价通
知，经双⽅采购部⻔协商后确认。此次涨价覆盖 EUV 与 DUV 光刻机的全部零部件，包括定期采
购的消耗性部件，以及因故障或性能下降⽽需更换的核⼼组件，涉及光学系统、光源、精密驱
动装置等。
此次全线⼀次性上调 10% 被业界视为异常举措。 ASML 此前通常仅在原材料价格⼤幅上涨或特定
材料采购困难时对个别零部件进⾏单独调价。与此同时， ASML 正与主要客⼾就新设备价格上调
展开谈判，设备本体涨价预期也在升温。业界普遍认为，三星和 SK 海⼒⼠接受涨价后，其他设

备与零部件供应商的提价要求将获得更⼤⽀撑。
ASML 零部件涨价：覆盖范围⼴，时机罕⻅
此次涨价范围涵盖 ASML 供应韩国市场的所有替换⽤设备零部件， EUV 与 DUV 光刻机均在其列。
从定期更换的消耗品到关键结构件，价格将于 2026 年 1 ⽉起统⼀上调 10% 。
ASML 将安装于客⼾⼯⼚的设备服务、零部件及升级业务单独核算。 2024 年该业务收⼊达 81.93
亿欧元，占公司总营收 326.67 亿欧元的约 25% 。 ASML 预计今年这部分收⼊将增⻓逾 30% 。
⼀位半导体⾏业⼈⼠表⽰，零部件构成要素的整体成本确实有所上升，但 ASML 此举也有在客⼾
盈利改善之际提升⾃⾝利润率的考量。
设备本体涨价谈判同步推进， TSMC 态度存分歧
在零部件之外，新订单设备的价格上调同样在酝酿之中。 ASML ⽬前正与主要客⼾就设备价格上
调进⾏磋商。
据 The Information 此前 7 ⽉报道， ASML 已通知中国半导体企业等客⼾， DUV 设备价格将上调
10% ，部分客⼾已接受。然⽽，作为 ASML 最⼤客⼾的台积电据悉对设备涨价持反对⽴场。

三星和 SK 海⼒⼠率先接受零部件涨价，被业界解读为⼀个重要信号 —— 在供需格局已然逆转的
背景下，⼤型客⼾的议价空间正在收窄。
涨价潮蔓延，多家设备巨头跟进
ASML 并⾮孤例。半导体设备领域的涨价趋势已在多家头部企业中蔓延。
美国应⽤材料公司在今年 8 ⽉的业绩发布中表⽰，新产品与现有产品价格均已提升。⽇本东京电
⼦在 7 ⽉末的业绩说明会上表⽰，正推进包括在售设备在内的价格调整，以应对成本上升压⼒。
美国等离⼦电源设备商 Advanced Energy 也宣布对部分产品线提价。

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

供需失衡是核⼼驱动⼒
此轮涨价潮的根本原因在于供需结构性失衡。 AI 数据中⼼投资热潮带动存储与晶圆代⼯⼚商同步
扩产，设备与零部件订单集中涌⼊，⽽供应商扩充产能需要时间。与此同时，通胀与原材料价
格上涨也推⾼了供应商的成本负担。
这与过去半导体⼚商凭借采购规模优势压低供应商单价的格局形成鲜明对⽐。当前，供应商的
价格谈判能⼒已显著增强。
⼀位⾏业⼈⼠指出，⼤规模扩产正在实际落地，设备与零部件已进⼊供应短缺阶段，随着晶圆
投⼊量持续增加，耗材领域也将出现⼤规模供应缺⼝。

2 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The Five Shocks of 2026—And the Extraordinary Resilience of the U.S. Economy
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
This is the year the consensus got mugged.
Rewind to the start of the year. The market consensus was that the U.S. economy was
set to slow. The Fed was set to cut interest rates. Corporate earnings were set to grow—

but not explode. Energy prices would remain tame. And the odds looked good that the
Republicans would control the House, the Senate, or both, following the midterm election.
Nine months on, the landscape looks starkly different. Defined by five shocks—energy,
interest rates, earnings, growth, and politics—2026 looks nothing like the year investors
thought they were buying on January 1.
Shock 1: Diesel Matters. While global oil prices have moved higher since the start of

the U.S./Iran war on February 28, oil prices have yet to surpass their previous record high
because the world has released some 400 million barrels from strategic reserves this year,
China has curbed its demand for oil imports, and the U.S. has goosed its energy exports to
record highs.
But here is the problem: While gasoline powers the American consumer, diesel powers the
American economy—and diesel prices are now sitting at record highs. Diesel supplies have

been hammered by wars in the Middle East and Ukraine that have sharply reduced refining
capacity. According to the International Energy Agency, by August, exports of diesel from
refiners in the Middle East had fallen by a quarter from prewar levels. Meanwhile, Ukraine
has hit 24 of Russia’s 34 largest refineries, reducing supplies from one of the world’s
largest diesel exporters. The upshot: U.S. diesel prices have soared from $3.55 a gallon
at the start of the year to roughly $6.52 a gallon. While crude oil trades for around $100

a barrel, the retail equivalent of a barrel of diesel now costs more than $260. The diesel
shock of 2026 was the last thing investors saw coming—and remains a key impediment to
structurally lower inflation.
Shock 2: From Interest Rate Cuts to Rate Hikes. Perhaps no other consensus view
entering 2026 has been turned more dramatically on its head than expectations for the
Fed. Heading into 2026, markets were pricing in rate cuts on expectations of a cooling

labor market, moderating inflation, and slower economic growth. The Fed easing cycle was
expected to continue in 2026. In January 2026, futures showed a roughly 50% chance of a
cut by March.
Expectations, however, flipped in late February with the start of the U.S./Iran war. The
Middle East energy shock reignited inflationary pressures, which, combined with stronger-
than-expected U.S. economic growth and a tightening labor market, have demolished the

odds of easier money in 2026. Short- and long-term Treasury yields rose sharply last week
in the wake of the S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index (PMI)
which pointed to the strongest business expansion in more than five years and the fastest
employment growth in over four years, while also reinforcing expectations that the Fed
will maintain a restrictive policy stance for longer. Markets were expecting two to three
quarter-point rate cuts in 2026; instead, the Fed has already raised rates once this year and

is expected to keep raising rates over the near term.
Shock 3: The Earnings Boom No One Saw Coming. Not all the shocks of 2026 are
negative. Indeed, stronger-than-expected corporate earnings have been among the most
positive factors of this year. Corporate America entered the year facing questions about
tariffs, AI-related capital expenditure spending, the health of the U.S. consumer, operating
leverage and the U.S. dollar outlook. At the start of the year, Wall Street was expecting

roughly $290 of S&P 500 earnings per share for 2026, per Bloomberg estimates. But by
September 21, the estimate had risen by $50, to $340 per share.
Investment Implications
While some of these shocks may
contribute to periods of market
volatility, the combination of strong
earnings growth, robust domestic
demand, and ongoing capital
investment suggests that the
fundamental backdrop for Equities
remains constructive.

### Page 3

3 of 8 September 28, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Earnings have been upgraded and underpinned by several factors, including robust final 
domestic demand in the U.S., a pickup in global growth, AI-related capital spending, 
productivity gains, and enhanced operating leverage. All these variables have helped deliver 
an earnings “shock” to Wall Street. And as an aside, yes, headline profit growth has been 
inflated by strong technology earnings and one-off investment gains from Amazon and 
Alphabet. That said, the S&P 500’s earnings story remains extraordinary. According to 
FactSet, the 493 companies excluding the Magnificent 7 1 
posted 31.8% earnings growth in 
Q2, the strongest level since late 2021.

Shock 4: An Economy with more Horsepower than the Headlines Suggest. That 
corporate profits have exceeded expectations this ye
ar is due in no small part to an economy 
that continues to surprise to the upside. The Fed was supposed to cut rates this year to 
keep the economy humming and prevent it from losing steam. But rather than limp through 
2026, the economy has leaped ahead. Yes, real gross domestic product (GDP) growth of 
2.1% in Q1 and 1.5% in Q2 are hardly blockbuster annualized rates of growth. However, the 
figures belie the underlying strength of the U.S. economy, with real final domestic demand

(which strips out trade) running at a 4.2% annual rate in Q2. According to the latest reading 
of the Atlanta Fed GDP tracker, Q3 real GDP grow
th is running at a red-hot rate of 5.1%. 
Slowdown? What slowdown?
Resilient consumer spending, robust capital investment, record leve
ls of new business 
formation, and strong foreign direct investment inflows have combined to stoke growth 
in the $32 trillion behemoth called the U.S. economy—the most resilient and dynamic 
economy in the world.
Shock 5: The Unexpected Washington Reset. The last shock is still in the making, but 
given current trends, the odds are rising that the Democrats could control both the House 
and Senate follow
ing the midterm elections. To this point, in January, and per Kalshi, the 
Democrats had a 71% shot at the House and an 18% chance of taking the Senate. Fast-
forward to today, and the odds are a 90% chance that the Democrats take the House and a 
60% chance that they take the Senate.
How all of this plays out remains to be seen—there are some 36 days before the November 
vote, and anything can happen. The market base case remains that the Democrats take the 
House, and the Senate is a toss-up—or too close to call. But with the Democrats expected to 
control at least one chamber of Congress, inv
estors can expect more volatility and uncertainty 
about negotiations ov
er the budget, debt ceiling, defense spending and AI regulation. Another 
political wildcard to watch: the changing dynamics within the Democratic Party.
The Biggest Shock of All for 2026? Pe
rhaps it’s not $6 diesel, rising interest rates, 
solid growth, booming profits or shifting politics. Rather, it’s the ability of the U.S. 
economy to absorb the unexpected, not buckle under stress, and continue generating  wealth 
for investors. To wit, the S&P 500 is on pace for its fourth
 consecutive year of double-digit 
returns.

MARKET VIEW
2026 Has, So Far, Defied the Traditional Midterm Year Playbook
Jordy Fuentes, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Current Landscape. To no surprise, market volatility persisted in 2026. Performance initially
tracked historical midterm election patterns before diverging meaningfully as the year
progressed, though there are still three months to go. Several items remain at hand as U.S.

voters head to the polls in November, including geopolitical and foreign policy uncertainty,
energy driven inflation pressures, affordability concerns, U.S. fiscal trajectory, and rapid
advancements in AI. Regardless of the eventual political outcome, history suggests that
markets and the economy can perform well across different configurations of government.
In our view, markets continue to be anchored by strong fundamentals and encouraging
seasonal trends.

To Be Addressed. U.S. domestic and foreign policy remain in focus as the global reorder,
tit-for-tat tariffs, and geopolitical conflicts spanning the Middle East to Eastern Europe have
added layers of uncertainty to the U.S. economic outlook.
At the same time, persistent geopolitical flare-ups have pushed oil prices materially
higher, adding to inflation pressures and keeping them above the Fed’s 2% target.
Headline Consumer Price Index (CPI) held above 3% year-over-year (YoY) in August, while

its core measure, which excludes food and energy, held at 2.4% YoY. The latest Personal
Consumption Expenditure (PCE), the Fed’s preferred measure of inflation, also remained
above 3% YoY for both its headline and core figures. 2
The U.S. fiscal trajectory is another area of focus. The U.S. budget deficit stands at 5.4% of
GDP , just above this century’s average of 4.6%. Meanwhile, U.S. public sector debt surpassed
the $40 trillion mark for the first time in August, oscillating around 100% of GDP .3 While these

trends warrant close monitoring, we view them as a slow-moving structural headwind rather
than an immediate concern, particularly within a highly innovative and diverse U.S. economy.
AI has emerged as a policy consideration, with debates increasingly centered on both
regulatory safeguards and the economic implications of the expanding infrastructure buildout.
For voters, those concerns extend to affordability, as the significant energy requirements of

new data centers raise questions about potential pressure on electricity costs.
Strap In for The Ride. Leading up to the midterm election, markets tend to experience
greater swings as investors navigate uncertainty surrounding the policy path for the
remainder of the presidential cycle (Exhibit 2A). Since 1930, the S&P 500 has averaged an
intrayear drawdown of 20% in midterm years, with the latest election in 2022 experiencing
a 25% intrayear pullback. This year, however, the S&P 500 largest peak-to-trough drawdown

has been 9%, considerably shallower than its long-term average (Exhibit 2B). Yet while
volatility may rise as election day approaches, 2026 has increasingly defied the traditional
midterm year playbook.
Why 2026 is Different. Despite entering a challenging stretch of the presidential cycle,
U.S. Equities have materially outpaced the typical year two pattern (Exhibit 2C). The S&P
500 experienced its best Q2 in a midterm year since 1938, surging 14.9% compared to an

average decline of 1.6%. From a monthly perspective, September stands out as the weakest
month, though month-to-date performance points to an unusual 1.0% rise. That resilience
follows a 2.6% gain in August, another month in which performance meaningfully exceeded
the historical decline of 0.5% (Exhibit 2D).
Through September, U.S. Equity resilience has also extended beyond headline returns with
both the frequency and magnitude of market pullbacks remaining relatively subdued. Market

corrections of 10% or more have occurred in just 29% of midterm election years since 1930.4
Against this backdrop, the market’s last correction came in spring 2025, while this year has
2 Bloomberg, Bureau of Labor Statistics. Data through August 31, 2026.
3 U.S. Treasury, Bureau of Economic Analysis. Data as of September 21, 2026.
4 Bloomberg, data through December 31, 2022.
Investment Implications
In our view, any related market
weakness could represent a

potential buying opportunity for
long-term investors.

seen only one 5%+ pullback compared to the average three per year, underscoring relatively
resilient market conditions.5 Year-to-date, the S&P 500 has increased 13%, well above the
average annual return of 3% according to Bloomberg through September 25, and on pace
to mark the strongest midterm election year since 2006. Taken together, 2026 has thus far
defied the traditionally weak midterm year seasonal pattern, reinforcing that every election

cycle is unique and historical trends may be a useful guide to navigate market volatility.
What’s Ahead? Forward performance is increasingly constructive for Equities. In the
post-war period, the S&P 500 rose 100% of the time in the six and 12 months following
elections, averaging 13% and 14%, respectively. 6 Even narrowing the time frame to three
months, markets ended higher 90% of the time. As investors push through the finish line,
the last three months of the year tend to be the strongest with October standing out,

ending higher 67% of the time. On average, Q4 has gained 5.6% since 1930, far surpassing
the preceding three quarters of election years. 7 Greater clarity about the U.S. fiscal policy
path serves as a potential tailwind for investors following the election.
To Take Home. Volatility tends to rise during midterm years, but history suggests that
reacting to short-term political uncertainty has rarely rewarded long-term investors. Over
longer horizons, the data suggests Equity returns and economic strength are shaped less

by election outcomes than by corporate profits, valuations, labor market conditions and
capital investments.

THE TICKER TAPE
What is the bond market telling us?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Office
We believe the bond market is telling us that the Fed is on track to meeting their dual
mandate of price stability and full employment. More importantly, when examining the
full yield curve the 2-year Treasury yield is currently around 4.88%, which typically is
the most sensitive to the fed funds rate and tends to price in future potential policy

adjustments. At the same time, the 10-year Treasury yield is currently about 5.11%. This
leads us to believe that the market expects the Fed to engineer a couple of more hikes
while the back end of the curve is indicating that economic growth is still healthy.
Taken together, it appears the full curve, which has clearly flattened, believes the Fed is
doing its job, while supply shocks, for obvious reasons, are not leading to higher long-run
inflation. The backup in long-dated yields is growth, not necessarily inflation as indicated

by rising “real” yields. Therefore, we view the drift higher in yields as a more normal sign
than what many have originally suggested. We continue to emphasize higher economic
growth for longer, rising productivity in the medium term, supply shortages to remain in
key areas within the AI space, and shocks in the energy equation. We expect the energy
shocks to ultimately fade away over time, but we do not believe that growth fades
anytime soon. In our view, a more normal bond market pricing in Fed moves at the short

end, and growth at the long end helps financial conditions and economic growth operate
in a more durable manner.
In summary, the front-end of the curve expects 3 to 4 more hikes in the next 18 months
or so. This can be debated obviously based on future data especially short-term energy
supply shocks. Long-end yields are drifting higher based on five main factors below, in
our view.
1. Strong economic growth (both
services and manufacturing)
2. Hawkish Fed

3. Debt and deficit concerns
4. Lower demand for long duration
sovereign debt in general
5. Energy supply shocks
The largest weight being strong economic activity which kick starts portfolio
repositioning particularly as the Fed hikes rates. The repositioning stops once demand
rises again when yields reach attractive levels for the more patient longer term
investment community. The supply shocks ultimately end and debt and deficit worries will
likely remain, but they still take a backseat for now. Longer duration government debt is

becoming more attractive each week even in the face of the concerns above. Be patient

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

S&P 500 Index Price Return is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States. A price return index is a stock
market index that tracks only the price changes and capital gains or losses of its component securities, without including dividends or cash distributions.
S&P Global Flash U.S. Composite Purchasing Managers’ Index is an early monthly economic indicator that estimates overall private-sector business activity by combining manufacturing

investment advisory services, including the type of advice and assistance provided, the fees charged, and the rights and obligations of the parties. It is important to understand the differences,
particularly when determining which service or services to select. For more information about these services and their differences, speak with your Merrill financial advisor.

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.

Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

### Page 1

MACROMICRO RESEARCH | FED WATCH
聯準會官員發⾔整理｜ 2026/10/12 （台灣時間）
涵蓋期間：近  14 天，美東時間  9/28 ⾄  10/11 。共整理  14 位官員，其中  9 位為  2026 年票委、 5 位為⾮
票委，並標注發⾔⽇期與鷹鴿⽴場。最後提出對政策路徑與資產配置的結論。
鷹鴿光譜⼀覽
粗體為  2026 年票委。
最鷹 Lorie Logan
鷹派 Michael Barr、Christopher Waller、 Alberto Musalem 、 Jeff Schmid
偏鷹 Neel Kashkari、Beth Hammack、Lisa Cook、 Austan Goolsbee
中性 John Williams、Philip Jefferson、 Mary Daly
偏鴿 （本期無）
未表態 Michelle Bowman、 Susan Collins
• 票委陣營：主張續升的票委有  3 位： Lorie Logan 估計⾄少再升  50 個基點， Michael Barr 認為需要「重新校

準」政策， Christopher Waller 預期再升息但不必連續會議動作。 Neel Kashkari 、 Beth Hammack 、 Lisa Cook
⽅向偏鷹，但對  10 ⽉時點保持開放； John Williams 與  Philip Jefferson 則明⾔「沒有急迫性」、需要更多時
間。 9 位發⾔票委沒有⼈主張降息，多數落在「⽅向向上、時點不急」， Kevin Warsh 近兩週未公開談政
策。
• ⾮票委鷹派：Alberto Musalem 主張  6–9 個⽉內再升、 Jeff Schmid 直⾔  AI 已是主要通膨推⼿、 Austan
Goolsbee 形容通膨久⾼「是在玩⽕」，⾮票委整體⽐票委更鷹；但他們今年沒有投票權，影響在於⽀撐  12
⽉與  2027 年初的升息定價，⽽⾮  10 ⽉的決策。
• 結論：市場已跟著票委的耐⼼訊號轉向。 CME 的  10 ⽉升息機率從  9/28 的  70.9% 降⾄  10/9 的  17.7% ，按兵
不動升⾄  82.3% ；但  12 ⽉累計升息機率仍有  82.7% ， 9 ⽉會議紀要也顯⽰多數官員認為年底前再升⼀次合

適。 10 ⽉暫停是延後，不是升息循環結束。
⼀、 2026 年票委（ 9 位，依鷹⾄鴿排列）

### Page 2

票委 最鷹
Lorie Logan   達拉斯  Fed 總裁
發⾔重點
10/1   於達拉斯  Fed 「 Voices of the Eleventh District 」活動開場致詞：估計利率⽬標區間需「再上調
50 個基點或更多」，⽬標是讓政策達到「溫和緊縮」。
10/1  9 ⽉升息是「重要的第⼀步」；⾄少再升幾碼，才能抵銷去年秋天共  75 個基點的「⾵險管理」
降息，「我們必須恢復物價穩定」。
10/1   通膨正朝  2.5% 左右前進，⽽⾮  2% ⽬標；失業率  4.1% 已接近多數估計的最低可持續⽔準，
勞動市場⼤致平衡。
10/1   ⻑天期殖利率近幾週明顯上升，較⾼的期限溢價可能拖慢經濟、減少所需的緊縮幅度；市場
本⾝也預期利率將⾛⾼。
10/2 、 10/6   先後在達拉斯  Fed 移⺠研討會致詞、於聖安東尼奧主持與前  BIS 總經理  Agustín
Carstens 的對談，均未⾒新的政策評論。
市場  10 年期美債殖利率當天盤中創  24 年新⾼後回落，收在  5.24% ； Bloomberg 以「需要更多升

息，但債市⾛勢可能幫上忙」解讀其發⾔。
票委 鷹派
Michael Barr  Fed 理事
發⾔重點
9/29   於底特律經濟俱樂部演講「經濟情勢與貨幣政策」：通膨達標的⾵險上升、勞動市場⾵險下
降，「需要重新校準政策」；基準情境下，可能還需要進⼀步調整政策，才能讓通膨及時回到⽬
標。
9/29   通膨已⾼於⽬標五年半；過去  20 個⽉只有  2 個⽉的核⼼  PCE 符合  2% ，「尚未看到及時回到
2% 的明確趨勢」，降溫進程被中東衝突與  AI 需求打亂。
9/29   勞動市場供需⼤致平衡，每⽉新增約  8 萬個職位、接近損益兩平，失業率  4.1% ；上半年
GDP 成⻑約  2% ，預期將略為加快。
9/29  AI 帶動晶⽚與相關設備價格飆升；未來  2–5 年最⼤的不確定性在⽣產⼒何時顯現、 AI 投資
重新定價與勞動替代，年輕勞⼯已出現早期跡象；持久的⽣產⼒提升可能推⾼中性利率，但「現
在判斷還太早」。
9/29   ⾼能源價格「仍在」，關稅的影響則可能已經減弱。

市場   同⽇  John Williams 表⽰不急， CME 的  10 ⽉升息機率由  9/28 的  70.9% 降⾄約  50% 。

Christopher Waller  Fed 理事

10/8   於伊斯坦堡經濟論壇演講「經濟預測摘要的訊號價值」：若數據符合預期，預期將再升息，
讓通膨更及時回到  2% ；時點有彈性，「不必連續會議升息」，但應在可接受的期間內到位。

增  0.25% 、年增  3% 。
10/8  AI 建置正顯著推升⾼科技消費品價格，建置規模的預估持續上修；專家警告低庫存與受損設
施可能讓油價⾼檔延續⾄  2027 年。
10/8  9 ⽉勞動市場「穩健且穩定」，⾮農增幅接近損益兩平；下半年經濟⾛強，不太擔⼼升息造成
破壞性放緩。

包含年初升息與其後降息。他主張以「訊號」指出利率的可能終點，⽽⾮逐次會議的前瞻指引。

推升商品價格，除⾮祭出新關稅。
9/29   勞動市場依然穩健，甚⾄在邊際上⾛強。

市場  10 ⽉升息機率由約  70% 降⾄約  50% （各媒體統計不⼀）， 2 年期殖利率下跌約  2.5 個基點。
10/6   主持紐約  Fed 治理與⽂化改⾰研討會，未談貨幣政策。
票委 中性
Philip Jefferson  Fed 副主席

10/1   於維吉尼亞⼤學  Darden 商學院演講「美國經濟與貨幣政策」：未來政策調整應仔細檢視數據
趨勢，形成判斷「可能需要更多時間」。
10/1   ⽀持  9 ⽉升息，認為這是確保⻑期通膨預期錨定的重要⼀步；將持續評估通膨能否以⾜夠速
度回到⽬標。
10/1  8 ⽉  PCE 年增  3.4% ，通膨⾼於  2% 已超過五年；通膨預測⾵險偏上、成⻑⾵險⼤致平衡；上
半年  GDP 年化成⻑  2.4% ，失業率  4.1% ，預期年底前維持在這個⽔準。
10/1   能源價格是近期整體通膨回升的主因，擔⼼擴散成更廣泛且持久的通膨；「我們沒有餘裕把
每個衝擊分開來看」。
10/1   企業投資「相當強勁」，主要靠  AI ⽀出⽀撐， AI 需求推升相關產品的⽣產成本；他也密切
關注  AI 對就業的影響。
市場   發⾔後  10 ⽉升息機率由約  35% 降⾄約  24% （週初約  70% ）， Reuters 以「 Fed 官員澆熄  10 ⽉
升息押注」形容。

債部位由約  6,000 億美元增⾄逾  7,000 億美元； eSLR 過去常成為約束，⽽⾮後盾。

年初上路；年內將考慮把  100 億美元等固定⾦額⾨檻指數化，並每五年檢討。

Alberto Musalem   聖路易  Fed 總裁（ 2028 年票委）
發⾔重點
10/8   於紐約的  Bloomberg 固定收益論壇表⽰，需要「進⼀步緊縮」才能讓通膨回到⽬標；若「及
時」約為  18 個⽉，利率應在未來  6–9 個⽉內上調。
10/8   未預設  10/28 會議結果，每次開會都持開放⼼態。

10/8   通膨由持續的需求壓⼒與供給衝擊共同推動，關鍵是及時回到  2% 並抑制第⼆輪效應；勞動
市場平衡穩定，無須冷卻就業來壓通膨；消費動能有消退⾵險。
10/8   ⾦融條件「溫和且有序」地收緊，但仍屬寬鬆、⽀撐成⻑；實質殖利率上升主要來⾃政策利
率預期，通膨預期仍錨定，未⾒  Fed 公信⼒受質疑。

### Page 6

10/8  AI 投資與政府⾚字推升殖利率；資本需求約占  GDP 3%–4% 、將持續  5–10 年，使利率⻑期
⾼於過往；美國財政多年處於不可持續路徑，貨幣政策獨⽴性是寶貴資產。
⾮票委 鷹派
Jeff Schmid   堪薩斯城  Fed 總裁（ 2028 年票委）
發⾔重點
10/1   於阿什維爾的鄉村投資研討會接受  Bloomberg 訪問： Fed 在通膨上「沒有兌現承諾」，引⽤主
席  Kevin Warsh 的話「我們還有⼯作要做」，形容通膨是「經濟竊賊」，必須⾼度聚焦。
10/6   通膨「令⼈沮喪，必須解決」，距  2% 還有很⻑的路； Fed 抗通膨的公信⼒正受考驗。
10/6  AI 已成為通膨的主要推⼿之⼀。
10/6   儘管⻑天期殖利率上升， Fed 在短端利率上仍有⼯作要做；勞動市場依然良好。
⾮票委 偏鷹
Austan Goolsbee   芝加哥  Fed 總裁（ 2027 年票委）
發⾔重點
9/29   通膨連續五年半⾼於⽬標「是在玩⽕」，需要重新檢討「看穿供給衝擊」的邏輯。

9/29   對未來  AI ⽣產⼒的預期使當下的過熱⾵險升⾼；龐⼤⾚字本⾝也是⼀種刺激，可能讓經濟
過熱。
9/29   聯準會法沒有要求讓債市滿意或避免股市意外；油價可能較快回落，但煉油產能恢復才是更
深層的問題；他在點陣圖中屬較樂觀的⼀⽅。
10/2   ⾮農公布當天接受  Fox Business 訪問： 10 ⽉升息或暫停「都可以放上桌」，希望先看到通膨
回到  2% 的證據；勞動市場穩定，通膨⾯更重要。
⾮票委 中性
Mary Daly   舊⾦⼭  Fed 總裁（ 2027 年票委）
發⾔重點
10/6   接受  Axios 專訪： 9 ⽉升息「完全必要」；若關稅、中東油價與  AI 等衝擊屬暫時性，「可能不
需要再升」，未表態是否⽀持再升息。
10/6  Fed 通常預期衝擊在  1–3 年內消退，但她擔⼼這次的緩解來得更晚。
10/6  AI 晶⽚需求「不像⼀次性」，需求不減反增；部分企業尋求記憶體遠期合約以鎖定供應， AI
需求可能排擠⾞⽤與家電晶⽚。
10/6   超⼤型雲端業者對利率「不太敏感」，但隨借款增加可能轉趨敏感；第⼆輪關稅將是「衝擊

之上再疊加衝擊」；將持續關注勞動市場。

### Page 7

發⾔重點
10/9   於密⻄根⼤學  John Leahy 紀念研討會致詞，追思其研究貢獻。

結論
數據更新⾄  2026-10-09 。
四⼤結論
1  10 ⽉按兵不動是基準情境， 12 ⽉再升⼀碼才是主路徑。 9 ⽉⾮農僅增  2.9 萬、失業率升⾄
4.2% 、時薪年增放緩⾄  3.0% ，給了  John Williams 、 Philip Jefferson 等票委等待的理由；但  9 ⽉會
議紀要顯⽰多數與會者認為年底前再升⼀次合適， Christopher Waller 也只是把升息的時點交給數
據決定。截⾄  10/9 ， CME 顯⽰  10 ⽉不動機率  82.3% 、 12 ⽉累計升息機率  82.7% 。投資上不宜為
降息預期定價， 3 個⽉期國庫券殖利率  4.25% 仍是等待資⾦的最佳停泊處，成⻑股的評價要靠盈
餘與現⾦流⽀撐，⽽⾮寬鬆預期。
2   暫停預期隨時可能逆轉，關鍵在油價與核⼼服務通膨。 10/9 布蘭特油價收在每桶  104.72 美元，

9 ⽉  ISM 服務業物價指數升⾄  74.0 、為  2022 年  7 ⽉以來新⾼，製造業物價指數  77.9 ，紐約  Fed
調查的⼀年期通膨預期升⾄  3.9% ，四項都指向通膨的第⼆輪效應。 10/14 公布的  9 ⽉  CPI 若核⼼
服務⾛強， 12 ⽉升息將被坐實、 10 ⽉升息機率也可能回升，這呼應  Lisa Cook 、 Philip Jefferson
對通膨擴散的警告。反向的條件是⾮農、零售與初領失業⾦同步轉弱，屆時衰退⾵險會取代通膨
⾵險，政策路徑才會轉向。
3   ⻑天期美債仍不宜承接，短天期國庫券是較佳的等待資⾦。 10 年期美債殖利率  10/5 收在
5.31% 、 10/7 盤中觸及約  5.36% 創  2002 年以來新⾼， 10/9 收在  5.24% ；同期  2 年期由  9/28 的
4.92% 回落⾄  4.80% ，顯⽰⻑端壓⼒主要來⾃期限溢價、財政與  AI 融資供給，⽽⾮政策路徑。
Alberto Musalem 估計  AI 帶動的資本需求約占  GDP 3%–4% 且將持續  5–10 年， Beth Hammack 更

直⾔殖利率上升無法取代升息。要等核⼼  PCE 連續降溫、⻑端殖利率不再創⾼、標售需求改善三
者同時出現，才是分批拉⻑存續期間的時機。
4   股票維持核⼼部位，但加碼⾨檻要提⾼；⿈⾦與美元各有分⼯。經濟仍在擴張、官員多認為勞
動市場撐得住升息，股票仍優於⻑債，但美股與  AI 供應鏈的加碼應以⾃由現⾦流、未履約訂單
（ RPO ）與資本報酬率驗證，避開依賴遠期現⾦流的⾼估值部位。⿈⾦  10/9 收在每盎司  4,216 美
元，適合維持  10%–15% 的分批避險部位，承擔財政與地緣⾵險，實質利率急升時暫緩加碼。美
元指數  10/9 為  102.21 、接近  10/5 創下的  52 週⾼點  102.54 ，保留美元短券作為流動性即可，不宜
因官員偏鷹就單邊押注美元⾛強。
近期觀察⽇曆

10/13 Christopher Waller （新德⾥  Bloomberg 論壇談  AI ）、 Susan Collins 、 Tom Barkin 發⾔

10/15 美國  9 ⽉  PPI 與零售銷售； Beth Hammack 再度發⾔； Kevin Warsh 於  IMF 年會與
Kristalina Georgieva 對談（美東  23:30 ，台灣  10/16 11:30 ）

10/15 晚間、台灣  10/16 上午。
2. 2026 年  FOMC 票委： 7 位理事、紐約  Fed 總裁，以及輪值的克⾥夫蘭、費城、達拉斯、明尼亞波利斯  4 位分⾏總
裁。⾮票委括號內為下次輪值年份。
3. 鷹鴿標記為本輪升息循環內的相對⽴場，僅依  9/28 起的發⾔判斷。 Beth Hammack 標為偏鷹，依據為  10/2 的「不
急」表態與  10/11 WSJ 報導中「⼩步、較頻繁升息」的偏好； Mary Daly 標為中性，因其明⾔「若衝擊屬暫時性可能
不需再升」。
4. 各媒體說法不⼀之處： Mary Daly 部分報導寫成「可能需要進⼀步緊縮」， Axios 原⽂則是她拒絕表態是否⽀持再升

息； John Williams 發⾔後的  10 ⽉升息機率變化有  70%→50% 與  64%→54% 兩種統計； Alberto Musalem 10/8 的場合
有  Bloomberg 論壇與明尼亞波利斯  Fed 活動兩說； Lorie Logan 「⾄少再升兩碼」是媒體由「 50 個基點或更多」推論
⽽來； Jeff Schmid 談  AI 的措辭有「主要推⼿之⼀」與「最⼤推⼿之⼀」兩種版本； Neel Kashkari 對  AI 的評論與中
性利率上修⾄  3.25% 主要⾒於韓⽂媒體轉述，本表未列中性利率數字； Beth Hammack 10/11 內容來⾃  WSJ 報導轉
述，⾮本⼈公開演講。
5. 近  14 天未查到貨幣政策公開發⾔⽽未列⼊： Kevin Warsh 、 Jerome Powell （仍為理事）、 Anna Paulson 、 Tom
Barkin ，以及亞特蘭⼤  Fed 代理總裁  Cheryl Venable 。窗⼝外的發⾔已排除，例如  John Williams 9/24 、 Anna Paulson

9/24 、 Susan Collins 9/23 、 Tom Barkin 9/22 。

認）； Michelle Bowman 10/14 ； Kevin Warsh 10/15 （美東）， 10/17 起進⼊靜默期，發⾔後需更新本表。

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: Shifted the focus back to energy power vendors again
Date:    9 hours ago
Shifted the focus back to energy power vendors again

Shifted the focus back to
energy power vendors again
在⾕歌與 Constellation 簽署兩份⻑期合約後，市場明顯將注意⼒轉
回電⼒能源產業
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
OCT 6 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
What’s New?
Google 與美國最⼤核電業者  Constellation Energy 達成指標性⻑期採購協
議，簽署  20 年期  43 億美元核能⼤單，總供電承諾達  3,590 MW 。

雙軌合約結構（增容  PPA + ⻑期基載電⼒）：
890 MW 功率增容  PPA：為期  20 年， Google 直接資助  Constellation 位
於伊利諾州、賓州與紐澤⻄州的  11 座現有核電機組進⾏翻新與技術升級（更
換蒸汽發⽣器、渦輪機與控制系統），相當於憑空增添⼀座⼤型反應爐的發電
容量，預計  2028 年起陸續供電⾄  2033 年全⾯上線。
2,700 MW 電網電⼒供應協議：為期  15 年，鎖定  PJM 電網內的穩定電⼒配
額（請參考附註了解 PJM 電網架構），為  Constellation 機組提供⻑期現⾦流
保障。
開創「⾃帶電⼒（ Bring Your Own Power, BYOP ）」防禦機制：針對資
料中⼼擴建引發「科技巨頭推⾼⺠⽣電費」的批評，本案採⽤  PJM 電網架
構，由  Google ⾃掏腰包承擔核電站設備升級的巨額資本⽀出，主打不將成本
轉嫁給⼀般居⺠⽤電戶。
科技巨頭核能策略可能轉向「既有設施增容與重啟」：繼微軟重啟三哩島

（ Three Mile Island ）、 Meta 採購  Clinton 核電廠電⼒、亞⾺遜合作  Talen

### Page 2

Energy 後， Google 也從⻑遠的  SMR （⼩型模組化反應爐）進⼀步落實到即
時可⾏的「既有核電廠增容」與「退役電廠重啟」路線。
To read the full post and support our work,
please consider becoming a paid subscriber.
Subscribed
What companies can be beneﬁted and why?
核電與獨⽴發電業者（ Independent Power Producers, IPP ）：
Constellation Energy (CEG)：最直接受益者，獲得確定性極⾼的  43 億
美元投資承諾與  20 年⻑期現⾦流，⼤幅提升估值倍數。（請參考之前深度報
告：What power energy names are beneﬁted more from 100GW
data center expansion? 8 names in comparison ）
Vistra (VST) 、 Talen Energy (TLNE) 、 PSEG：全美⾮受監管核電與基

載電⼒資產被全⾯重估，從過去低成⻑的公⽤事業轉型為  AI 時代具戰略溢價
的稀缺基載資產。
重型發電與核電設備供應商（ Power & Industrial OEMs ）：
GE Vernova (GEV) 、 Westinghouse （ Cameco / Brookﬁeld 持
股）、⻄⾨⼦能源（ Siemens Energy ）：核電增容（ Uprates ）的核⼼在於
更換汽輪機、⼤型變壓器、蒸氣發⽣器與數位監控系統，直接帶動重型電⼒設
備供應鏈龐⼤訂單。
核燃料供應鏈（ Uranium Miners & Enrichment）：
Cameco (CCJ) 、 Centrus Energy (LEU) 、 Uranium Energy (UEC) ：機組
增容運轉及電廠延役延⻑了燃料消耗週期，讓天然鈾（ U3 O8 ）與低濃縮鈾
（ LEU ）的⻑期結構性需求更加穩固。
超⼤規模雲端巨頭（ Google / Alphabet ）：
在  PJM 這個全美資料中⼼最密集且電網負載最吃緊的區域，提前鎖定⻑達  20

年的無碳基載電⼒，避免因電網壅塞⽽被迫延後  AI 伺服器機房上線，同時捍
衛其  2030 年  24/7 零碳排（ CFE ）的承諾。
What companies can be negatively aﬀected and
why?
缺乏儲能的純綠能業者（ Pure-play Wind / Solar Developers ）：

### Page 3

AI 資料中⼼要求  99.999% 的  24/7 恆定供電。純⾵光發電的間歇性在科技巨
頭預算配置中逐漸失寵，科技公司的購電優先順序正轉向核能與地熱，傳統未
配儲能的純光伏 / ⾵電開發商的議價空間遭到壓縮，除⾮再配合儲能系統⼀起打
包出售。
⼆線雲端業者與中⼩規模資料中⼼業者（ Tier-2 CSPs & Mid-size
Colocations ）：
電⼒正取代晶⽚成為算⼒擴張的最⼤瓶頸。巨頭憑藉數⼗億美元級別的資產負
債表包下龐⼤發電量，⼆線廠商在  PJM 等熱⾨電網拿不到⾜夠並網容量，⾯
臨嚴峻的「電⼒排擠效應」。
區域內⾼耗能的傳統製造業（鋼鐵、重化⼯業）：
儘管增容部分的建設由  Google 出資，但⾼達  2,700 MW 的電⼒合約仍鎖定
了電網中龐⼤的供給資源。隨著整體區域備載容量緊縮， PJM 容量拍賣
（ Capacity Auction ）價格⽔漲船⾼，可能進⼀步推⾼當地傳統⼯廠的⽤電
成本。
Our outside-the-box thinking
「核電增容」正在實質截胡  SMR （⼩型模組化反應爐）的⿈⾦窗⼝期：

市場共識普遍認為  SMR 是解決  AI 供電的終極救星，但  SMR ⾯臨審批週期
⻑、⾸台商業化成本過⾼（ FOAK ⾵險）的挑戰。 Constellation 此次透過
11 座現有反應爐增容  890 MW ，直⾔這相當於「在更短時間內憑空建出好幾
座  SMR 」。這意味著在  2028 〜 2035 年間，「增容  + 重啟舊機組」⽬前是科
技業解決⽅案，甚⾄可能使部分商業化進度落後的  SMR 新創公司⾯臨資⾦被
抽離的⽣存危機。
「⾃帶電⼒（ BYOP ）」表⾯上避免推⾼⺠⽣電價，實質上在將公共電網「階
級私有化」：
儘管科技業標榜⾃費升級電廠不影響⼀般⽤電戶，但背後本質是頂級科技資本
以  20 年⻑約包下全美最優質、最穩定的無碳基載發電能⼒。當優質核電被矽
⾕巨頭鎖定，公共電網與⼀般⼤眾將被迫分攤⾼波動、依賴天然氣或間歇性⾵
光的發電⾵險。這在未來數年極可能引發嚴重的監管審查與社會反壟斷反彈
（ Regulatory & Social Backlash ）。
科技巨頭正演變為「影⼦綜合能源公⽤事業（ Shadow Utilities ）」：

Google 這次不是被動向公⽤事業買電，⽽是扮演「電廠重⼤資本改造的出資
⽅兼推⼿」。科技公司的資產負債表已直接穿透⾄發電基礎設施底層。未來衡

### Page 4

量⼀間  AI 超⼤規模雲端巨頭的核⼼壁壘，可能不再單看演算法參數或  GPU 庫
存，⽽是「能源專案融資與物理鎖定基載電⼒的能⼒」。
極端能源溢價將反向倒逼「算⼒能效⾰命」與「端側 / 邊緣  AI 」超預期爆發：
當鎖定幾座資料中⼼的電⼒需動輒砸下  40~50 億美元與  20 年承諾時，雲端
暴⼒堆疊參數量（ Brute-force Scaling ）的邊際經濟效益正在逼近物理極
限。這將逼迫產業加速尋找替代⽅案：更極致的稀疏化模型（ MoE ）、光學計
算晶⽚，以及能將計算負載分散⾄終端設備的「邊緣 / 端側  AI （ Edge AI ）」，
這反⽽可能成為下⼀輪顛覆雲端資料中⼼商業模式的⾮共識催化劑。
附註：什麼是  PJM 電網架構
PJM （ PJM Interconnection ）  是全美規模最⼤、運作機制最成熟的區域輸電
組織（ RTO, Regional Transmission Organization ），負責調度與協調美國
東部及中⻄部  13 個州以及華盛頓特區的⾼壓電網與電⼒批發市場。

其名稱源⾃最初結盟的三個州： Pennsylvania （賓⼣法尼亞）、 Jersey （紐澤
⻄）、 Maryland （⾺⾥蘭）。如今，包含北維吉尼亞州（全球最密集的資料中
⼼聚落  Data Center Alley ）都在  PJM 的管轄範圍內。
⼀、  PJM 的組織⾓⾊與定位
PJM 本⾝不擁有電廠、不擁有電網，也不從事電⼒買賣。它是⼀個受美國聯邦
能源監管委員會（ FERC ）監管的獨⽴⾮營利實體，核⼼⾓⾊有⼆：
電網可靠度調度員（ Grid Reliability Operator ）：控制超過  84,000 英⾥的
⾼壓輸電線路，秒級平衡區域內  6,500 萬⼈的⽤電需求與發電供給。
批發電⼒市場管理者（ Market Operator ）：營運透明、競爭性的電⼒批發市
場，媒合獨⽴發電廠（如核電、燃氣、⾵光發電商）與電⼒零售商 / ⼤⽤電戶。
⼆、  PJM 的三⼤市場架構
PJM 的電⼒市場運作主要依賴三⼤核⼼機制：
電能市場（ Energy Market ）：

分為⽇前市場（ Day-Ahead ）與即時市場（ Real-Time ）。
採⽤分區邊際定價（ LMP, Locational Marginal Pricing ）。 LMP 不僅反映發
電成本，還包含輸電線路壅塞成本（ Congestion ）與線路損耗（ Losses ）。
若某區域（如維吉尼亞機房區）⽤電需求暴增且輸電線路壅塞，當地的  LMP
就會飆⾼。

### Page 5

容量市場（ Capacity Market / RPM, Reliability Pricing Model ）：
這是  PJM 最具特⾊的機制。為確保「未來  3 年」電網在尖峰負載時絕不斷
電， PJM 每年舉⾏容量基礎拍賣（ BRA, Base Residual Auction ）。
發電廠競標承諾在未來特定年份提供⾜夠的備⽤容量，得標者即使不發電，也
能按⽇領取「保證在場費」。近來因⽼舊電廠退役與  AI 負載激增， PJM 容量拍
賣清算價格屢創新⾼。
輔助服務市場（ Ancillary Services Market ）：
採購調頻（ Frequency Regulation ）、備轉容量（ Spinning Reserves ）等
服務，確保電網在發電機跳機或負載突增時，能在數秒⾄數分鐘內維持電網頻
率（ 60Hz ）穩定。
三、  為什麼  AI 巨頭與核電交易都繞不開  PJM ？
當前科技巨頭（微軟、 Google 、 Meta 、亞⾺遜）的核電採購與⼤型資料中⼼
擴建，⾼度集中在  PJM 範圍內，主因在於其獨特的戰略位置與市場結構：

考，並⾮投資建議，個⼈應審慎評估⾵險，如因此造成任何損失，本⼈不負任
何擔保責任 ; We are here to provide our professional opinions on
company and industry analysis but not the investment advice. The
use of the information contained in this material is at your own risk.
We make no guarantees and assume no responsibility or liability for
any information on the report.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
WESTPAC NOWCAST Q3
2026: FIRST ESTIMATE
Growth picks up despite headwinds
25 September 2026
Key points
• Westpac-Now points to GDP growth
of around 0.6%qtr in Q3 2026 (with a
70% confidence interval of 0.29%qtr to
0.91%qtr) and 2.3%yr. This is a step up

from the 0.4%qtr and 2.1%yr recorded
in Q2 2026 (see here) and implies that
economic momentum has strengthened.
•  Our Monthly Activity Index has
stabilised after declining sharply since
November 2025 and is now showing
signs of improvement in July. This
provides further evidence the economy
is proving more resilient than initially
feared. Rather than deteriorating further,
activity appears to be weathering the
impacts of the Middle East conflict and

higher interest rates relatively well.
•  The RBA is unlikely to be comfortable with
this combination of improving conditions
and firmer economic momentum. Growth
at 2.3%yr would be close to our estimate
of ‘trend’, but it is above the RBA’s estimate
at a time when the Bank is trying to
engineer a period of below-trend growth
to ensure inflation returns to target.
• Overall, the results are consistent with
our view that the economy is being
supported by powerful business investment

tailwinds, centred on data centres
and renewable energy (see here). This
investment surge is largely unrelated to
the domestic business cycle or interest
rates and is increasingly offsetting
weakness elsewhere in the economy.

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
In November 2025, we introduced Westpac-Now (Westpac
Nowcast (Westpac-Now): The Upswing Accelerates |
Westpac IQ), a real time measure of current activity in the
Australian economy. Westpac-Now draws on more than
60 high frequency economic and financial variables, using

advanced statistical techniques to provide a timely pulse
check on economic momentum. It is the first Australian
model to incorporate internal banking data, enhancing its
predictive power.
This note provides the first estimate of GDP growth for the
September quarter 2026, using domestic and international
data released up to and including the August Labour Force
Survey (released 24 September 2026). Future updates will be
published monthly (see Appendix A for the release schedule).

Westpac Monthly Activity Index: Activity improves
as momentum builds
After weakening sharply through late 2025 and early 2026,
the Westpac Monthly Activity Index is beginning to recover.
While activity levels remain subdued, recent readings suggest
the economy has moved beyond the period of broad-based
deterioration seen earlier in the year. On a rolling three-month
basis, the Index has continued to improve, consistent with
improving momentum.
The weakness through late 2025 and early 2026 reflected

a broad-based deterioration across confidence, spending,
business activity and labour market indicators as households
and businesses adjusted to a renewed inflation challenge
followed by interest rates moving higher once again. More
recent data suggest these conditions have begun to stabilise
Economic momentum building
Pat Bustamante
Senior Economist
and, in some cases, improve. Business and consumer
confidence, household spending, and labour market indicators

have generally proven more resilient than expected given the
headwinds facing the economy.
This improvement is evident in our heatmap, where the
intensity and breadth of negative readings have moderated
noticeably. While pockets of softness remain, particularly
in interest-sensitive areas of the economy, fewer indicators
are showing deteriorating momentum and a growing
number are now contributing positively to activity. Business-
related indicators remain among the strongest contributors,

consistent with the substantial support currently being
provided by investment in data centres, renewable energy and
related infrastructure.
Our indicator heatmap decomposes changes in the Monthly
Activity Index into group-level contributions, allowing us to
assess which components are driving movements in activity.
For ease of interpretation, indicators are grouped into broad
categories (see the Appendix for further details). For example,
the labour market category includes both official ABS Labour

Force Survey outcomes and timelier survey-based measures
of labour demand and job advertisements.
Westpac-Now: Resilient growth amid rising headwinds
Recall that Westpac-Now is a refined indicator that combines
our Monthly Activity Index with advanced econometric
techniques to estimate GDP growth. As previously noted,
the fit is not expected to be exact. Measured GDP includes
components that are not well captured by high-frequency
activity indicators, such as imputed rents for owner-occupiers,

housing related ownership transfer costs, public and business
inventories, and depreciation.

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
To bridge monthly and quarterly data, we use a MIDAS
framework, allowing us to combine mixed-frequency inputs
and incorporate partial information for the current quarter
when producing GDP forecasts.
Based on current model estimates, Westpac-Now’s final

estimate for Q2 2026 points to GDP growth of 0.6%qtr
(with a 70% confidence interval of 0.29%qtr to 0.91%qtr)
and 2.3%yr, representing a step up from the 0.4%qtr and
2.1%yr outcomes recorded in Q2 2026.  This suggests that
economic momentum has strengthened, with the economy
continuing to show resilience.
The key question going forward is whether higher interest
rates, elevated energy prices and heightened geopolitical
uncertainty in the Middle East will begin to weigh more

heavily on demand, offsetting the substantial support
currently being provided by business investment. Our
forecasts suggest that growth will moderate, particularly
following the RBA Board’s September rate hike, with activity
slowing rather than stalling.
Looking ahead, model dynamics suggest growth will pick up
towards trend, at around 0.6%qtr in Q4 2026 and Q1 2027.
However, these projections remain much more tentative,
given their reliance on projected inputs rather than realised

data. In addition, heightened uncertainty, particularly around
the lingering impacts of the Middle East conflict, means risks
around these forward estimates are larger than usual.
September Quarter data: Momentum improving
Data released in the first month of the September quarter
indicates that the economy has picked up momentum relative
to the previous quarter, with our nowcast estimating Q3
growth at 0.6%qtr, in line with the 0.6%qtr “pure forecast”

based solely on data available up to June 2026.
This is similar to what occurred in Q2 2026. Following the
large downside surprise in April 2026, which coincided with
sharp falls in consumer and business confidence, growth
estimates stabilised over the remainder of the quarter. The
weakness in Q2 was concentrated in April, with subsequent
data pointing to stabilisation rather than further deterioration.
Early Q3 data point to a similar pattern, with activity showing

signs of resilience despite headwinds.
Note that monthly activity estimates are more uncertain than
normal when inflation is shifting. Faster price growth can cause
nominal indicators, such as consumer spending, to rise more
strongly even though ‘real’, inflation-adjusted spending may
be softening (and vice versa). The inclusion of ‘real’ indicators,
such as employment and banking transaction volumes, helps
mitigate some – but not all – of this uncertainty.

Bottom line:
Westpac-Now points to GDP growth of around 0.6%qtr in
Q3 2026 (2.3%yr), indicating that the economy remains more
resilient than previously feared. Strong business investment,
particularly in data centres and renewable energy, continues to
provide an important offset to soft demand in interest-sensitive
sectors of the economy, including the housing market.
Looking ahead, higher interest rates, elevated energy prices
and ongoing geopolitical uncertainty are expected to weigh

on activity, particularly following the RBA’s September rate
hike. However, our central case remains one of slower growth
rather than a material downturn.

Appendix A: Westpac-Now Release Schedule (2026)
Going forward, Westpac Now will be published on the Friday following each ABS Labour Force Survey (LFS) release, at 11:30am AEDT/AEST (Canberra time).
Each release incorporates the latest labour market data alongside other high frequency indicators and provides an updated nowcast for quarterly GDP growth.

As the quarter progresses, each update should be interpreted as a progressively more complete estimate of that quarter’s economic performance.
Release date (Friday) LFS reference month Quarter focus Interpretation
16 October 2026 September 2026 Q3 2026 – second estimate Refined Q3 signal as activity data accumulate
20 November 2026 October 2026 Q3 2026 – final estimate Near complete read on Q3 activity
18 December 2026 November 2026 Q4 2026 – first estimate Initial assessment of Q4 momentum

《通膨帶動升息壓力，美元走強》
美元從2026年初降息交易轉向受到美伊戰爭 、油價
上漲及通膨預期升溫支撐，但此波強勢主要來自地緣
政治風險及利率維持高檔 。從9月Fed季度經濟預測
可見，經濟成長展望維持在 2.5%以下溫和成長 ，通
膨中長期則預期回穩但2026可能繼續維持在3%以上，
因此4Q26美國貨幣政策將繼續有升息的壓力 。從聯
準會點陣圖及 ECB後續政策路徑觀察 ，市場對歐美
利差擴大的預期對美元有利。然而，歐日景氣動能

3
《歐元投資關鍵-歐元區經濟活動加速擴張》
7月以來，北約示警灰色地帶模糊戰的風險升溫，導
致歐元短線偏弱 ，歐洲資金明顯轉向瑞郎進行避險 。
然而，歐洲基本面此時卻持續改善。歐元區9月景氣
加速擴張 ，綜合PMI初值創41個月新高 ，服務業與
製造業同步改善 ，其中製造業產出創 55個月高點。
德國成長動能增強 ，法國則10個月來首度恢復擴張 。
新訂單連續第3個月成長，增速為2022年5月以來最
快，出口訂單亦持續回升，帶動未完成訂單自2022
年6月以來首度增加。另一方面，中東衝突推升能源

價格，使投入成本與售價增速升至近4個月高點。整
體而言，歐元區經濟韌性優於預期 ，但通膨升溫與
企業信心偏弱仍是後續關注焦點。
貨幣政策方面 ，歐洲央行官員持續釋出偏鷹訊號 ，
認為能源價格再度上漲將推升並延長通膨壓力 ，未
來數月歐元區通膨可能接近4%。愛爾蘭央行行長表
示不排除10月再度升息，強調每次會議皆有行動可
能。首席經濟學家 Lane指出第 2輪能源衝擊將帶動
食品與商品價格上漲 。多位官員認為經濟仍具韌性 ，
為進一步收緊政策提供空間 ，市場也預期未來一年
歐洲央行仍有多次升息機會 ，以防止通膨預期根深
蒂固。基本面繼續支持 ECB升息的環境下 ，預計美
歐利差收斂將提供歐元支撐。4Q26目標價1.19。

元年底目標1.34
4
《英鎊投資關鍵-英國央行維持利率3.75%不變》
9月經濟數據方面，9月PMI連續第3個月擴張，但成
長動能放緩。綜合PMI初值創6月以來新低，製造業
與服務業活動同步降溫 。其中，服務業受內需疲弱
及地緣政治不確定性拖累；製造業則受惠於 AI投資
與國防支出增加 ，但消費需求疲弱仍構成壓力 。通
膨壓力持續升溫，企業投入成本創6月以來最高，並
推升企業售價增幅 。英國經濟維持低速擴張 ，GDP
季增率僅約0.1%，但成本與通膨壓力升高 ，反映經
濟成長疲弱與通膨升溫並存的局面。
貨幣政策方面，9月央行維持利率3.75%不變，為成

熟國家少數維持利率不變的國家，英鎊兌美元創3個
月新低。央行總裁貝里表示 ，能源成本上升對英國
物價和薪資影響有限 ，但若這種波動持續下去 ，未
來有可能需要調高基準利率 。目前消費者物價指數
為3.1%，低於4月央行預測值 ，實際通膨展望低於
預期提供央行維持貨幣政策的基調 。展望後續，歐
非中東地緣政治情勢短期仍處於高不確定性階段 ，
並且導致近期天然氣價格飆升 ，英國仍有升息壓力 。
英國貨幣政策短線持穩 ，而經濟數據面則小幅改善 ，
但財政政策不確定性風險大幅削弱英鎊的投資誘因 ，
維持英鎊中性評等，英鎊兌美元年底目標1.34。

《瑞郎投資關鍵-瑞士維持0利率，瑞郎創16個月新
低》
9月瑞士央行將利率維持在0%，但小幅上修通膨預期。
2026年通膨從0.6%上修至0.7%，2027-28年預期為
0.8%。瑞士通膨及成長前景溫和 ，央行並未因地緣
政治風險而強調通膨壓力，經濟成長方面則因化學和
製藥行業表現強勁，2026年GDP成長預期介於1.5%-
2%，2027年則維持預估1.5%。整體而言，利差擴大
為瑞郎走弱主因。央行對升息態度保守，外需回溫與
製造業改善正帶動瑞士經濟展望持續好轉。然而，全
球收益率上升，市場對於美歐升息路徑上修，利差擴
大對瑞郎不利，短線瑞郎受避險需求及利差影響面臨

盤整格局，2H26美元兌瑞郎維持目標價0.8。

重點摘要
• 日本干預匯市動用15.4兆日圓
《日圓投資關鍵-日圓回貶但資金動能大幅轉正》
美元兌日圓自6-7月維持在160以上，7月底站上164
後大幅拉回 ，2026年7-8月份日本干預匯市共動用
15.4兆日圓，創2022年以來最高金額。8月美日聯手
干預日圓以來一度回測 160，但9月1日再度從160.4
快速下探至152.9，兩周日圓升值幅度近5%。市場揣
測日本政府年金投資基金(GPIF)基金(1.85兆美元全球
最大養老基金)進場購買日本公債。同時，美國財長
貝森特示警日圓空頭交易，表示｢掌握不對稱資訊，
現在莊家是我｣。加上日本公布7月名目現金薪資年增
4.7%，創29年最大升幅，日本央行貨幣政策正常化
路徑延續。貝森特支持日本以美國公債作為抵押，讓

日本在不出售美國公債的情況下買入日圓 (FIMA-外
國與國際貨幣當局附買回機制)。目前日本持有1.1兆
美元公債，同時加上美國政府表態支持干預弱勢日圓，
美日聯手讓日圓短線貶勢暫時告一段落。美元兌日圓
9月在160再度形成一波回檔，顯示美日政府力守160
整點關卡的決心。目前日本利率(無擔保隔夜拆款利
率)1.25%水準再同時加入日圓隔夜指數交換利率隱
含升息3碼，2027年利率水準與歐美差距仍有一段距
離，實質利率仍為負值，且在成熟市場中偏低。除此
之外，高市政府擴張性財政政策及新增支出計畫恐持
續不利日圓。高市於7月30日宣布自2027年4月起，
將現行8%食品消費稅率降至1%，實施期限為2年，

聯友金屬 7610 TT (綠能環保) 
供應跟不上需求擴大 
投資與評價 
1. 3Q26 受惠國際鎢供應持續吃緊、鎢價維持高檔，加上屏東二廠新增
30%產能陸續開出，自售比重持續提升，預估營收季增 47.5%，毛利率
66.5%，EPS 27.32 元創單季新高。 
2. 2027 年成長動能由鎢價轉向擴產與產品高值化，屏南三廠、屏科廠陸
續開出，產品由鎢酸鈉延伸至 APT、氧化鎢、鎢粉及碳化鎢，自售比重
有機會挑戰 60%，預估 2026/27 EPS 83.77/109.27 元。 
3. 目前股價評價位於近兩年本益比 10～24 倍區間中段，考量非中鎢供應
鏈稀缺性、自售比重提升及 2027 年新產能接棒，給予區間中間偏上的
22 倍 FY27E PER，給予強力買進評等，目標價 2,400 元(22x FY27E 
EPS)。

重點更新 
3Q26 鎢價與自售業務推升獲利續增 
預估 3Q26 營收 28.43 億元（+47.5% QoQ） ，主要受惠國際鎢價維持高檔、
鎢酸鈉自售業務成長，以及屏東二廠新增 30%產能陸續開出；毛利 18.90 億
元（+44.8% QoQ） ，毛利率66.5%（-1.3ppts QoQ） ，營收與毛利仍維持高
速成長 。 營業費用1.88 億元 （+55.6% QoQ） ， 稅後淨利13.27 億元 （+40.3% 
QoQ），EPS 27.32 元創單季新高。預估 4Q26 隨新產能進一步量產、自售比
重過半，營收29.59 億元 （+4.1% QoQ） ，惟費用投入增加，EPS 降至 26.17
元。 
2027 年產能與高值化產品接棒 
預估 2027 年營收 132.79 億元 （+52.1% YoY） ，成長動能由鎢價逐步轉向產
能擴張及產品高值化，屏南三廠與屏科廠新產能陸續開出，產品由鎢酸鈉延
伸至 APT、氧化鎢、鎢粉及碳化鎢，自售比重有機會挑戰 60%；毛利 87.22
億元（+48.9% YoY） ，毛利率65.7%（-1.4ppts YoY） 。惟新廠與新工序投入
增加 ， 營業費用20.62 億元 （+202.8% YoY） ， 使營業利益66.60 億元 （+28.7% 
YoY） 、 營益率50.2%（-9.1ppts YoY） ； 稅後淨利53.09 億元 （+30.4% YoY），
EPS 109.27 元再創年度新高。後續營運重點將轉向新產能利用率，以及高值
化產品的提升速率。
